```php
            . ' entry_count INT NOT NULL, story_count INT NOT NULL, duration_ms INT NOT NULL,'
            . ' peak_memory_bytes INT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (time_window)) DEFAULT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4');
        $this->addSql('CREATE TABLE trending_story (id INT AUTO_INCREMENT NOT NULL, time_window VARCHAR(8) NOT NULL,'
            . ' first_seen DATETIME NOT NULL, last_seen DATETIME NOT NULL, outlet_count INT NOT NULL,'

            . ' INDEX idx_trending_story_entry_feed (feed_id), PRIMARY KEY (story_id, entry_id))'
            . ' DEFAULT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4');

            . ' FOREIGN KEY (story_id) REFERENCES trending_story (id) ON DELETE CASCADE');
        $this->addSql('ALTER TABLE trending_story_entry ADD CONSTRAINT FK_8C835665BA364942'

    private function upOnSqlite(): void
    {
        $this->addSql('CREATE TABLE trending_snapshot (time_window VARCHAR(8) NOT NULL, built_at DATETIME NOT NULL,'
            . ' entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL, story_count INTEGER NOT NULL, duration_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,'
            . ' peak_memory_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (time_window))');
        $this->addSql('CREATE TABLE trending_story (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT NOT NULL,'
            . ' time_window VARCHAR(8) NOT NULL, first_seen DATETIME NOT NULL, last_seen DATETIME NOT NULL,'
            . ' outlet_count INTEGER NOT NULL)');
        $this->addSql('CREATE INDEX idx_trending_story_window_outlets ON trending_story (time_window, outlet_count)');
        $this->addSql('CREATE TABLE trending_story_entry (feed_id INTEGER NOT NULL, story_id INTEGER NOT NULL,'
            . ' entry_id INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (story_id, entry_id),'
            . ' CONSTRAINT FK_8C835665AA5D4036 FOREIGN KEY (story_id)'
            . ' REFERENCES trending_story (id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT DEFERRABLE INITIALLY IMMEDIATE,'
            . ' CONSTRAINT FK_8C835665BA364942 FOREIGN KEY (entry_id)'
            . ' REFERENCES entry (id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT DEFERRABLE INITIALLY IMMEDIATE)');
        $this->addSql('CREATE INDEX IDX_8C835665AA5D4036 ON trending_story_entry (story_id)');
        $this->addSql('CREATE INDEX IDX_8C835665BA364942 ON trending_story_entry (entry_id)');
        $this->addSql('CREATE INDEX idx_trending_story_entry_feed ON trending_story_entry (feed_id)');

    private function abortIfUnsupportedPlatform(): void
    {

        $this->abortIf(
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Repository;

use App\Entity\LinkCard;
use App\Entity\LinkCardContent;
use App\Repository\TrendingEntryRepository;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\WindowEntryModel;
use App\Tests\DbTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingFixtures;

final class TrendingEntryRepositoryTest extends DbTestCase
{
    public function testStreamsTheWindowsEntriesInIdOrderWithTheirFirstRootLinkCard(): void
    {
        $fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager);
        $feed = $fixtures->feed('stream');
        $tooOld = $fixtures->entry($feed, 'Too old', '2031-05-13 11:59:59');
        $first = $fixtures->article($feed, 'First', '2031-05-13 12:00:00', 'https://news.example.com/first');
        $second = $fixtures->entry($feed, 'Second', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $second->setSummary('<p>Body</p>');
        $fixtures->flush();
        $this->entityManager->persist(new LinkCard($second, 1, self::card('https://later.example.com/b')));
        $this->entityManager->persist(new LinkCard($second, 0, self::card('https://shared.example.com/a')));
        $this->entityManager->flush();

        $rows = iterator_to_array(
            (new TrendingEntryRepository($this->entityManager))->windowEntries(
                new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-13 12:00:00', new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
            ),
            false,
        );

        self::assertSame(
            [$first->requireId(), $second->requireId()],
            array_map(static fn (WindowEntryModel $row): int => $row->id, $rows),
        );
        self::assertNotContains($tooOld->requireId(), array_map(static fn (WindowEntryModel $row): int => $row->id, $rows));
        self::assertSame($feed->requireId(), $rows[0]->feedId);
        self::assertSame($first->getUrlHash(), $rows[0]->urlHash);
        self::assertNull($rows[0]->linkCardUrl);
        self::assertSame('https://shared.example.com/a', $rows[1]->linkCardUrl);
        self::assertSame('<p>Body</p>', $rows[1]->summary);
        self::assertSame('2031-05-14 09:00:00', $rows[1]->effectiveDate->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        self::assertSame('UTC', $rows[1]->effectiveDate->getTimezone()->getName());
    }

    private static function card(string $url): LinkCardContent
    {
        return new LinkCardContent($url, 'Card', null, null, 'example.com');
    }
}
```

Which change catches what: `11:59:59` vs `12:00:00` straddle `>=`; the card at position 1 is inserted first, so a
query that takes "any card" instead of the lowest position returns `later…/b`; a missing UTC zone fails the last
assertion. Wrap the `assertNotContains` line to 120 characters when writing the file.

`backend/tests/Repository/TrendingSnapshotRepositoryTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Repository;

use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\TrendingSnapshotRepository;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\StoryMemberModel;

use App\Tests\DbTestCase;

final class TrendingSnapshotRepositoryTest extends DbTestCase
{
    public function testReplacingAWindowWritesItsStoriesMembersAndSnapshot(): void
    {
        [$one, $two] = $this->twoEntries();

        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Day, 2), [self::story($one, $two)]);
        $this->entityManager->clear();

        self::assertSame(1, $this->storyCount(TrendingWindow::Day));
        self::assertSame(2, $this->memberCount());
        $snapshot = $this->entityManager->find(TrendingSnapshot::class, 'day');
        self::assertInstanceOf(TrendingSnapshot::class, $snapshot);
        self::assertSame(7, $snapshot->getEntryCount());
        self::assertSame(2, $snapshot->getStoryCount());
        self::assertSame(311, $snapshot->getDurationMilliseconds());
        self::assertSame(4_096_000, $snapshot->getPeakMemoryBytes());
    }

    public function testReplacingADayLeavesTheWeekAndDropsTheOldDay(): void
    {
        [$one, $two] = $this->twoEntries();
        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Day, 1), [self::story($one, $two)]);
        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Week, 1), [self::story($one, $two)]);

        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Day, 0), []);

        self::assertSame(0, $this->storyCount(TrendingWindow::Day));
        self::assertSame(1, $this->storyCount(TrendingWindow::Week));
        self::assertSame(2, $this->memberCount(), 'the dropped day story took its members with it');
    }

    public function testPurgingAnEntryRemovesItsStoryLink(): void
    {
        [$one, $two] = $this->twoEntries();
        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Day, 1), [self::story($one, $two)]);

        $this->entityManager->getConnection()->executeStatement('DELETE FROM entry WHERE id = ?', [$one]);

        self::assertSame(1, $this->memberCount());
    }

    public function testBuiltAtIsReadBackAsUtcPerWindow(): void
    {
        $this->snapshots()->replace(self::report(TrendingWindow::Week, 0), []);

        $builtAt = $this->snapshots()->builtAtByWindow();

        self::assertSame(['week'], array_keys($builtAt));
        self::assertSame('2031-05-14T12:00:00+00:00', $builtAt['week']->format(\DateTimeInterface::ATOM));
    }

    /** @return array{int, int} */
    private function twoEntries(): array
    {
        $fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager);
        $one = $fixtures->entry($fixtures->feed('one'), 'One', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $two = $fixtures->entry($fixtures->feed('two'), 'Two', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $fixtures->flush();

        return [$one->requireId(), $two->requireId()];
    }

    private static function story(int $one, int $two): TrendingStoryModel
    {
        return new TrendingStoryModel(
            [new StoryMemberModel($one, 101), new StoryMemberModel($two, 202)],
            new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 08:00:00', new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
            new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 09:00:00', new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
        );
    }

    private static function report(TrendingWindow $window, int $storyCount): TrendingBuildReportModel
    {
        return new TrendingBuildReportModel(
            $window,
            new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 12:00:00', new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
            7,
            $storyCount,
            311,
            4_096_000,
        );
    }

    private function snapshots(): TrendingSnapshotRepository
    {
        $repository = $this->entityManager->getRepository(TrendingSnapshot::class);
        self::assertInstanceOf(TrendingSnapshotRepository::class, $repository);

        return $repository;
    }

    private function storyCount(TrendingWindow $window): int
    {
        return (int) $this->entityManager->getConnection()
            ->fetchOne('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trending_story WHERE time_window = ?', [$window->value]);
    }

    private function memberCount(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->entityManager->getConnection()->fetchOne('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trending_story_entry');
    }
}
```

The feed ids `101`/`202` in `story()` are deliberately not the entries' real feeds: `feed_id` is a copied column
without a foreign key (D20), and the repository writes what the model says.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the repositories**

`backend/src/Repository/TrendingEntryRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Repository;

use App\Service\Trending\Model\WindowEntryModel;
use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;

/** The rebuild's read: a window's entries with only the columns Trending matches on, a chunk at a time (#2017). */
final readonly class TrendingEntryRepository
{
    private const int CHUNK_ROWS = 1_000;

    /** A summary is cut to 300 characters after its markup is stripped; this bounds what a chunk holds before that. */
    private const int SUMMARY_CHARACTERS = 2_000;

    public function __construct(private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager)
    {
    }

    /** @return iterable<WindowEntryModel> every entry dated at or after $since, in id order */
    public function windowEntries(\DateTimeImmutable $since): iterable
    {
        $afterId = 0;
        do {
            $rows = $this->chunkAfter($since, $afterId);
            foreach ($rows as $row) {
                $entry = self::modelOf($row);
                $afterId = $entry->id;
                yield $entry;
            }
        } while (\count($rows) === self::CHUNK_ROWS);
    }

    /**
     * @return list<array{
     *   id: int|string, feed_id: int|string, title: string, title_derived: bool|int|string, summary: string|null,
     *   effective_date: string, url_hash: string|null, link_card_url: string|null
     * }>
     */
    private function chunkAfter(\DateTimeImmutable $since, int $afterId): array
    {
        /**
         * @var list<array{
         *   id: int|string, feed_id: int|string, title: string, title_derived: bool|int|string,
         *   summary: string|null, effective_date: string, url_hash: string|null, link_card_url: string|null
         * }> $rows
         */
        $rows = $this->entityManager->getConnection()->fetchAllAssociative(
            \sprintf(
                'SELECT e.id, e.feed_id, e.title, e.title_derived, SUBSTR(e.summary, 1, %d) AS summary,'
                . ' e.effective_date, e.url_hash,'
                . ' (SELECT card.url FROM link_card card WHERE card.entry_id = e.id AND card.thread_post_id IS NULL'
                . ' ORDER BY card.position LIMIT 1) AS link_card_url'
                . ' FROM entry e WHERE e.effective_date >= :since AND e.id > :afterId ORDER BY e.id LIMIT %d',
                self::SUMMARY_CHARACTERS,
                self::CHUNK_ROWS,
            ),
            ['since' => $since->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'), 'afterId' => $afterId],
        );

        return $rows;
    }

    /**
     * @param array{
     *   id: int|string, feed_id: int|string, title: string, title_derived: bool|int|string, summary: string|null,
     *   effective_date: string, url_hash: string|null, link_card_url: string|null
     * } $row
     */
    private static function modelOf(array $row): WindowEntryModel
    {
        return new WindowEntryModel(
            (int) $row['id'],
            (int) $row['feed_id'],
            $row['title'],
            (bool) (int) $row['title_derived'],
            $row['summary'],
            new \DateTimeImmutable($row['effective_date'], new \DateTimeZone('UTC')),
            $row['url_hash'],
            $row['link_card_url'],
        );
    }
}
```

Executor: the three-way repeated row shape may be lifted into one `@phpstan-type WindowEntryRow` on the class
docblock and imported in each place.

`backend/src/Repository/TrendingSnapshotRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Repository;

use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\StoryMemberModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingBuildReportModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryModel;
use Doctrine\Bundle\DoctrineBundle\Repository\ServiceEntityRepository;
use Doctrine\DBAL\Connection;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;

/**
 * The one writer of the Trending tables: a rebuild replaces one window's stories and its snapshot row together.
 *
 * @extends ServiceEntityRepository<TrendingSnapshot>
 */
final class TrendingSnapshotRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    /** Rows per INSERT: 3 placeholders each, kept under SQLite's historical 999. */
    private const int INSERT_ROWS = 300;

    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, TrendingSnapshot::class);
    }

    /** @return array<string, \DateTimeImmutable> built_at in UTC, by window value; a window never built is absent */
    public function builtAtByWindow(): array
    {
        /** @var list<array{time_window: string, built_at: string}> $rows */
        $rows = $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()
            ->fetchAllAssociative('SELECT time_window, built_at FROM trending_snapshot');

        $builtAt = [];
        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            $builtAt[$row['time_window']] = new \DateTimeImmutable($row['built_at'], new \DateTimeZone('UTC'));
        }

        return $builtAt;
    }

    /**
     * Deleting the window's stories cascades to their members. The caller wraps this in its transaction, so readers
     * keep the old snapshot until the commit (#2017).
     *
     * @param list<TrendingStoryModel> $stories
     */
    public function replace(TrendingBuildReportModel $report, array $stories): void
    {
        $connection = $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection();
        $connection->executeStatement('DELETE FROM trending_story WHERE time_window = ?', [$report->window->value]);
        foreach ($stories as $story) {
            $this->insertStory($connection, $report->window, $story);
        }
        $connection->executeStatement('DELETE FROM trending_snapshot WHERE time_window = ?', [$report->window->value]);
        $connection->insert('trending_snapshot', [
            'time_window' => $report->window->value,
            'built_at' => $report->builtAt->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'entry_count' => $report->entryCount,
            'story_count' => $report->storyCount,
            'duration_ms' => $report->durationMilliseconds,
            'peak_memory_bytes' => $report->peakMemoryBytes,
        ]);
    }

    private function insertStory(Connection $connection, TrendingWindow $window, TrendingStoryModel $story): void
    {
        $connection->insert('trending_story', [
            'time_window' => $window->value,
            'first_seen' => $story->firstSeen->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'last_seen' => $story->lastSeen->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'outlet_count' => $story->outletCount(),
        ]);
        $storyId = (int) $connection->lastInsertId();
        foreach (array_chunk($story->members, self::INSERT_ROWS) as $members) {
            $this->insertMembers($connection, $storyId, $members);
        }
    }

    /** @param non-empty-list<StoryMemberModel> $members */
    private function insertMembers(Connection $connection, int $storyId, array $members): void
    {
        $parameters = [];
        foreach ($members as $member) {
            array_push($parameters, $storyId, $member->entryId, $member->feedId);
        }
        $connection->executeStatement(
            \sprintf(
                'INSERT INTO trending_story_entry (story_id, entry_id, feed_id) VALUES %s',
                implode(', ', array_fill(0, \count($members), '(?, ?, ?)')),
            ),
            $parameters,
        );
    }
}
```

`$connection` passed between two private methods of one class is decomposition, not tramp data (`minClasses` 2).

### Task 6: The rebuild — builder, `rebuildDue()`, worker message and maintenance tick

Decisions: D4, D5, D21, D22, D23.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Trending/Pass/BuildStopwatch.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingGrouping.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingSnapshotBuilder.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingRebuild.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Trending/Model/TrendingRebuildReportModel.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Worker/Message/RebuildDueTrendingStories.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Worker/Handler/RebuildDueTrendingStoriesHandler.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Maintenance/DerivedViewSweeps.php`
- Create: `backend/src/Service/Maintenance/Model/DerivedViewsReportModel.php`
- Modify: `backend/src/Service/Worker/WorkerSchedule.php` (append last)
- Modify: `backend/src/Service/Maintenance/MaintenanceTick.php`
- Modify: `backend/src/Service/Maintenance/Model/MaintenanceSweepsModel.php`
- Modify: `backend/src/Http/MaintenanceTickJson.php`
- Modify: `backend/tests/Support/TrendingWiring.php` (Task 3 created it with `storyLinks()`; add the rest, keep `storyLinks()` as is)
- Test: `backend/tests/Service/Trending/TrendingSnapshotBuilderTest.php`, `backend/tests/Service/Trending/TrendingRebuildTest.php`,
  `backend/tests/Service/Trending/TrendingContainerWiringTest.php`,
  `backend/tests/Service/Worker/RebuildDueTrendingStoriesHandlerTest.php`,
  `backend/tests/Service/Maintenance/DerivedViewSweepsTest.php`; modify
  `backend/tests/Service/Worker/WorkerScheduleWiringTest.php`, `backend/tests/Service/Maintenance/MaintenanceTickTest.php`,
  `backend/tests/Http/MaintenanceTickJsonTest.php`

**Why `DerivedViewSweeps`:** `MaintenanceTick`'s constructor already takes 9 parameters; a 10th (`TrendingRebuild`)
trips PHPMD's ExcessiveParameterList. The tick's membership sweep and the Trending rebuild are both views recomputed
from the entries, so they move together into one collaborator, the way `FollowUpFetchSweeps` groups the follow-up
fetches, and `MaintenanceSweepsModel::$savedSearchMemberships` becomes `$derivedViews` (still 8 parameters). The
tick now runs the private-feed sync *before* the membership sweep, so that the Trending rebuild comes after every
existing sweep (D23); nothing reads the order between those two.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TrendingWindow` (Task 1); `HeadlineFiller`, `HeadlineWindow` (Task 2); `TrendingRun`, `StoryLinks`,
  `WindowEntryModel` (Task 3); `StoryGrouping` (Task 4); `TrendingEntryRepository::windowEntries()`,
  `TrendingSnapshotRepository::{builtAtByWindow,replace}()`, `TrendingStoryModel`, `StoryMemberModel`,

`backend/tests/Support/TrendingWiring.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Support;

use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Repository\TrendingEntryRepository;
use App\Repository\TrendingSnapshotRepository;
use App\Service\Clock\NaiveUtcClock;
use App\Service\Trending\HeadlineFiller;
use App\Service\Trending\StoryGrouping;
use App\Service\Trending\StoryLinks;
use App\Service\Trending\StoryLinkSource\HeadlineLinks;
use App\Service\Trending\StoryLinkSource\SharedUrlLinks;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingGrouping;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingRebuild;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingSnapshotBuilder;
use App\Service\Url\UrlNormalizer;
use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;
use Symfony\Component\DependencyInjection\ServiceLocator;

/**
 * The Trending job over the test database and a caller's clock. Built by hand: the container inlines the private
 * services used once, so the test container cannot hand them out.
 */
final class TrendingWiring
{
    public static function rebuild(EntityManagerInterface $entityManager, ClockInterface $clock): TrendingRebuild
    {
        $naiveClock = new NaiveUtcClock($clock);
        $snapshots = self::snapshots($entityManager);
        $builder = new TrendingSnapshotBuilder(
            self::grouping($entityManager, $clock),
            $snapshots,
            $entityManager,
            $naiveClock,
        );

        return new TrendingRebuild($builder, $snapshots, $naiveClock);
    }

    public static function grouping(EntityManagerInterface $entityManager, ClockInterface $clock): TrendingGrouping
    {
        return new TrendingGrouping(
            new TrendingEntryRepository($entityManager),
            new UrlNormalizer(),
            new HeadlineFiller(),
            self::storyLinks(),
            new StoryGrouping(),
            new NaiveUtcClock($clock),
        );
    }

    public static function snapshots(EntityManagerInterface $entityManager): TrendingSnapshotRepository
    {
        $repository = $entityManager->getRepository(TrendingSnapshot::class);
        if (!$repository instanceof TrendingSnapshotRepository) {
            throw new \LogicException('trending_snapshot has no TrendingSnapshotRepository.');
        }

        return $repository;
    }

    private function __construct()
    {
    }
}
```

`backend/tests/Service/Trending/TrendingSnapshotBuilderTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Trending;

use App\Entity\LinkCard;
use App\Entity\LinkCardContent;
use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Enum\SocialNetwork;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Service\Clock\NaiveUtcClock;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingSnapshotBuilder;
use App\Tests\DbTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingFixtures;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingWiring;
use Symfony\Component\Clock\MockClock;

final class TrendingSnapshotBuilderTest extends DbTestCase
{
    private const string NOW = '2031-05-14 12:00:00';

    private TrendingFixtures $fixtures;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager);
    }

    public function testGroupsTheDaysHeadlinesIntoAStoryAndRecordsTheSnapshot(): void
    {
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('alpha'), 'Hafenstreik legt Hamburg lahm', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('beta'), 'Hafenstreik in Hamburg: Terminals lahm', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('gamma'), 'Hamburg: Hafenstreik macht Terminals lahm', '2031-05-14 10:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('delta'), 'Wetter morgen sonnig', '2031-05-14 11:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('epsilon'), 'Hafenstreik Hamburg lahm', '2031-05-13 11:59:59');
        $this->fixtures->flush();

        $report = $this->builder()->build(TrendingWindow::Day);
        $this->entityManager->clear();

        self::assertSame(4, $report->entryCount, 'the entry a second before the window is not read');
        self::assertSame(1, $report->storyCount);
        self::assertSame(
            [['outlet_count' => 3, 'first_seen' => '2031-05-14 08:00:00', 'last_seen' => '2031-05-14 10:00:00']],
            $this->storedStories(TrendingWindow::Day),
        );
        self::assertSame(3, $this->memberCount());
        $snapshot = $this->entityManager->find(TrendingSnapshot::class, 'day');
        self::assertInstanceOf(TrendingSnapshot::class, $snapshot);
        self::assertSame(self::NOW, $snapshot->getBuiltAt()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        self::assertSame(4, $snapshot->getEntryCount());
        self::assertGreaterThan(0, $snapshot->getPeakMemoryBytes());
    }

    public function testAGroupOfTwoFeedsIsTooNarrowForTheDay(): void
    {
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('alpha'), 'Hafenstreik legt Hamburg lahm', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('beta'), 'Hafenstreik in Hamburg: Terminals lahm', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->flush();

        self::assertSame(0, $this->builder()->build(TrendingWindow::Day)->storyCount);
    }

    public function testPostsSharingAnArticleJoinItsStoryThroughTheirLinkCards(): void
    {
        $this->fixtures->article(
            $this->fixtures->feed('news'),
            'Etwas ganz anderes',
            '2031-05-14 08:00:00',
            'https://news.example.com/streik?utm_source=feed',
        );
        foreach (['ann', 'ben', 'cat'] as $index => $handle) {
            $post = $this->fixtures->entry(
                $this->fixtures->feed($handle, SocialNetwork::Bluesky),
                ['Lesenswert', 'Wichtig!', 'Seht euch das an'][$index],
                \sprintf('2031-05-14 %02d:00:00', 9 + $index),
            );
            $this->fixtures->flush();
            $this->entityManager->persist(new LinkCard(
                $post,
                0,
                new LinkCardContent('https://news.example.com/streik', 'Streik', null, null, 'news.example.com'),
            ));
        }
        $this->fixtures->flush();

        $report = $this->builder()->build(TrendingWindow::Day);

        self::assertSame(1, $report->storyCount);
        self::assertSame(4, $this->memberCount());
    }

    public function testRebuildingTheDayLeavesTheWeekAlone(): void
    {
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('alpha'), 'Hafenstreik legt Hamburg lahm', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('beta'), 'Hafenstreik in Hamburg: Terminals lahm', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('gamma'), 'Hamburg: Hafenstreik macht Terminals lahm', '2031-05-14 10:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('delta'), 'Hafenstreik: Hamburg lahm, Terminals zu', '2031-05-14 10:30:00');
        $this->fixtures->entry($this->fixtures->feed('omega'), 'Hamburg Terminals lahm: Hafenstreik', '2031-05-14 10:45:00');
        $this->fixtures->flush();
        $this->builder()->build(TrendingWindow::Week);

        $this->builder()->build(TrendingWindow::Day);

        self::assertCount(1, $this->storedStories(TrendingWindow::Week));
        self::assertCount(1, $this->storedStories(TrendingWindow::Day));
    }

    private function builder(): TrendingSnapshotBuilder
    {
        $clock = new MockClock(self::NOW, 'UTC');

        return new TrendingSnapshotBuilder(
            TrendingWiring::grouping($this->entityManager, $clock),
            TrendingWiring::snapshots($this->entityManager),
            $this->entityManager,
            new NaiveUtcClock($clock),
        );
    }

    /** @return list<array<string, mixed>> */
    private function storedStories(TrendingWindow $window): array
    {
        $rows = $this->entityManager->getConnection()->fetchAllAssociative(
            'SELECT outlet_count, first_seen, last_seen FROM trending_story WHERE time_window = ?',
            [$window->value],
        );

        return array_map(
            static fn (array $row): array => ['outlet_count' => (int) $row['outlet_count']] + $row,
            $rows,
        );
    }

    private function memberCount(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->entityManager->getConnection()->fetchOne('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trending_story_entry');
    }
}
```

`backend/tests/Service/Trending/TrendingRebuildTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Trending;

use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingBuildReportModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingRebuildReportModel;
use App\Tests\DbTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingWiring;
use Symfony\Component\Clock\MockClock;

final class TrendingRebuildTest extends DbTestCase
{
    public function testBuildsEveryWindowNeverBuilt(): void
    {
        self::assertSame([TrendingWindow::Day, TrendingWindow::Week], self::windowsOf($this->rebuildDue()));
    }

    public function testRebuildsAWindowOnceItsSnapshotIsAsOldAsItsInterval(): void
    {
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Day, '2031-05-14 11:50:00');
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Week, '2031-05-14 11:00:00');

        self::assertSame([TrendingWindow::Day, TrendingWindow::Week], self::windowsOf($this->rebuildDue()));
    }

    public function testLeavesASnapshotYoungerThanItsIntervalAlone(): void
    {
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Day, '2031-05-14 11:50:01');
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Week, '2031-05-14 11:00:01');

        self::assertSame([], self::windowsOf($this->rebuildDue()));
    }

    public function testEachWindowKeepsItsOwnInterval(): void
    {
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Day, '2031-05-14 11:40:00');
        $this->snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow::Week, '2031-05-14 11:40:00');

        self::assertSame([TrendingWindow::Day], self::windowsOf($this->rebuildDue()));
    }

    private function rebuildDue(): TrendingRebuildReportModel
    {
        return TrendingWiring::rebuild($this->entityManager, new MockClock('2031-05-14 12:00:00', 'UTC'))->rebuildDue();
    }

    private function snapshotBuiltAt(TrendingWindow $window, string $builtAt): void
    {
        $this->entityManager->persist(new TrendingSnapshot($window, new \DateTimeImmutable($builtAt), 0, 0, 0, 0));
        $this->entityManager->flush();
    }

    /** @return list<TrendingWindow> */
    private static function windowsOf(TrendingRebuildReportModel $report): array
    {
        return array_map(static fn (TrendingBuildReportModel $build): TrendingWindow => $build->window, $report->builds);
    }
}
```

Which change each value catches: 600 s / 599 s and 3 600 s / 3 599 s straddle `>=`; a 20-minute-old pair proves the
day and week intervals are read per window (one interval for both fails).

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the Trending job**

`backend/src/Service/Trending/Pass/BuildStopwatch.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending\Pass;

/** One build's wall time and peak memory, both recorded with its snapshot (#2017). */
final readonly class BuildStopwatch
{
    private function __construct(private int $startedAtNanoseconds)
    {
    }

    /** Resets the process's peak-memory mark, so the peak read later is this build's own. */
    public static function start(): self
    {
        memory_reset_peak_usage();

        return new self((int) hrtime(true));
    }

    public function elapsedMilliseconds(): int
    {
        return intdiv((int) hrtime(true) - $this->startedAtNanoseconds, 1_000_000);
    }

    public function peakMemoryBytes(): int
    {
        return memory_get_peak_usage();
    }
}
```

`backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingGrouping.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\TrendingEntryRepository;
use App\Service\Clock\NaiveUtcClock;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\StoryMemberModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\WindowEntryModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Pass\HeadlineWindow;
use App\Service\Trending\Pass\TrendingRun;
use App\Service\Url\UrlNormalizer;

/** Reads one window's entries and groups them into stories, in memory; writing them is the builder's (#2017). */
final readonly class TrendingGrouping
{
    public function __construct(
        private TrendingEntryRepository $windowEntries,
        private UrlNormalizer $urlNormalizer,
        private HeadlineFiller $filler,
        private StoryLinks $storyLinks,
        private StoryGrouping $storyGrouping,
        private NaiveUtcClock $clock,
    ) {
    }

    public function collect(TrendingWindow $window): TrendingRun
    {
        $run = new TrendingRun($window, new HeadlineWindow($this->filler));
        $since = $this->clock->now()->modify(\sprintf('-%d seconds', $window->spanSeconds()));
        foreach ($this->windowEntries->windowEntries($since) as $entry) {
            $run->add($entry, $this->urlHashesOf($entry));
        }

        return $run;
    }

    /** @return list<TrendingStoryModel> every group, however few feeds it spans */
    public function groups(TrendingRun $run): array
    {
        return array_map(
            static fn (array $entryIds): TrendingStoryModel => self::storyOf($run, $entryIds),
            $this->storyGrouping->group($run, $this->storyLinks->between($run)),
        );
    }

    /** @return list<TrendingStoryModel> the groups spanning enough feeds that some reader could see them trend */
    public function stories(TrendingRun $run): array
    {
        return array_values(array_filter(
            $this->groups($run),
            static fn (TrendingStoryModel $story): bool => $story->outletCount() >= $run->window->minSubscribedFeeds(),
        ));
    }

    /** @return list<string> */
    private function urlHashesOf(WindowEntryModel $entry): array
    {
        return array_values(array_unique(array_filter(
            [$entry->urlHash, $this->urlNormalizer->hash($entry->linkCardUrl)],
            static fn (?string $urlHash): bool => $urlHash !== null,
        )));
    }

    /** @param list<int> $entryIds two or more */
    private static function storyOf(TrendingRun $run, array $entryIds): TrendingStoryModel
    {
        $members = array_map(
            static fn (int $entryId): StoryMemberModel => new StoryMemberModel($entryId, $run->feedOf($entryId)),
            $entryIds,
        );
        $times = array_map($run->effectiveTimeOf(...), $entryIds);

        return new TrendingStoryModel($members, self::instant(min($times)), self::instant(max($times)));
    }

    private static function instant(int $unixSeconds): \DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return new \DateTimeImmutable('@' . $unixSeconds);
    }
}
```

Executor: PHPStan needs `$members` as `non-empty-list` for `TrendingStoryModel`; `StoryGrouping::group()` documents
groups of two or more — tighten its return to `list<non-empty-list<int>>` (or `list<list<int>>` with an
`\assert($entryIds !== [])`) so the types line up; `min()`/`max()` on a possibly-empty array are flagged the same way.

`backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingSnapshotBuilder.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\TrendingSnapshotRepository;
use App\Service\Clock\NaiveUtcClock;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingBuildReportModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Pass\BuildStopwatch;
use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;

/** Groups one window and replaces its snapshot in one transaction: readers see the old one until the commit. */
final readonly class TrendingSnapshotBuilder
{
    public function __construct(
        private TrendingGrouping $grouping,
        private TrendingSnapshotRepository $snapshots,
        private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
        private NaiveUtcClock $clock,
    ) {
    }

    public function build(TrendingWindow $window): TrendingBuildReportModel
    {
        $stopwatch = BuildStopwatch::start();
        $run = $this->grouping->collect($window);
        $stories = $this->grouping->stories($run);
        $report = new TrendingBuildReportModel(
            $window,
            $this->clock->now(),
            \count($run->entryIds()),
            \count($stories),
            $stopwatch->elapsedMilliseconds(),
            $stopwatch->peakMemoryBytes(),
        );
        $this->entityManager->wrapInTransaction(fn () => $this->snapshots->replace($report, $stories));

        return $report;
    }
}
```

`backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingRebuild.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\TrendingSnapshotRepository;
use App\Service\Clock\NaiveUtcClock;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingRebuildReportModel;

/** Rebuilds every window whose snapshot is missing or as old as its interval; the worker and the tick call it. */
final readonly class TrendingRebuild
{
    public function __construct(
        private TrendingSnapshotBuilder $builder,
        private TrendingSnapshotRepository $snapshots,
        private NaiveUtcClock $clock,
    ) {
    }

    public function rebuildDue(): TrendingRebuildReportModel
    {
        $builtAt = $this->snapshots->builtAtByWindow();
        $builds = [];
        foreach (TrendingWindow::cases() as $window) {
            if ($this->isDue($window, $builtAt[$window->value] ?? null)) {
                $builds[] = $this->builder->build($window);
            }
        }

        return new TrendingRebuildReportModel($builds);
    }

    private function isDue(TrendingWindow $window, ?\DateTimeImmutable $builtAt): bool
    {
        return $builtAt === null
            || $this->clock->now()->getTimestamp() - $builtAt->getTimestamp() >= $window->rebuildIntervalSeconds();
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Maintenance;

use App\Service\Maintenance\Model\DerivedViewsReportModel;
use App\Service\Search\Membership\Model\SweepBudgetModel;
use App\Service\Search\Membership\SavedSearchMembershipSweep;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingRebuildReportModel;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingRebuild;
use Psr\Clock\ClockInterface;

/** The tick's last sweeps: they recompute views of the entries the earlier sweeps may have changed. */
final readonly class DerivedViewSweeps
{
    /** The membership sweep's own cap inside the tick window: enough for ~50 chunks on Strato. */
    private const int MEMBERSHIP_BUDGET_SECONDS = 10;

    public function __construct(
        private SavedSearchMembershipSweep $membershipSweep,
        private TrendingRebuild $trendingRebuild,
        private ClockInterface $clock,
    ) {
    }

    public function sweep(\DateTimeImmutable $deadline): DerivedViewsReportModel
    {
        $budget = SweepBudgetModel::remainingUntil($deadline, $this->clock->now(), self::MEMBERSHIP_BUDGET_SECONDS);
        $memberships = $this->membershipSweep->sweep($budget);

        return new DerivedViewsReportModel($memberships, $this->trendingWhileTimeIsLeft($deadline));
    }

    private function trendingWhileTimeIsLeft(\DateTimeImmutable $deadline): TrendingRebuildReportModel
    {
        if ($this->clock->now() >= $deadline) {
            return TrendingRebuildReportModel::none();
        }

        return $this->trendingRebuild->rebuildDue();
    }
}
```

```php
    public function testAPairFailingOnlyTheWeightIsANearMissWithItsNumbers(): void
```

`HeadlineLinks` — add `use App\Service\Trending\Model\NearMissModel;`, a constant `private const int HOUR = 3_600;`
and:

```php
    /** @return list<NearMissModel> pairs of different feeds sharing a headline stem that fail exactly one rule */
    public function nearMisses(TrendingRun $run): array
    {
        $nearMisses = [];
        foreach ($run->entryIds() as $entryId) {
            foreach ($this->overlapsWithEarlier($run, $entryId) as $overlap) {
                $failures = $this->failuresOf($run, $overlap);
                if (\count($failures) === 1 && !$run->sameFeed($overlap->earlierEntryId, $overlap->entryId)) {
                    $nearMisses[] = new NearMissModel($overlap, $failures[0]);
                }
            }
        }

        return $nearMisses;
    }

    /** @return list<string> */
    private function failuresOf(TrendingRun $run, HeadlineOverlapModel $overlap): array
    {
        $failures = [];
        if (!$run->withinGap($overlap->earlierEntryId, $overlap->entryId)) {
            $failures[] = \sprintf(
                'gap %.1f h > %g h',
                $run->gapSeconds($overlap->earlierEntryId, $overlap->entryId) / self::HOUR,
                $run->window->maxLinkGapSeconds() / self::HOUR,
            );
        }
        if (!$this->weighsEnough($overlap)) {
            $failures[] = \sprintf('shared weight %.1f < %g', $overlap->sharedWeight, self::MIN_SHARED_WEIGHT);
        }
        if (!$this->sharesEnough($overlap)) {
            $failures[] = \sprintf(
                'share of the lighter entry %.2f < %g',
                $overlap->shareOfLighter(),
                self::MIN_SHARE_OF_LIGHTER,
            );
        }

        return $failures;
    }
```

`backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingExplainer.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingExplanationModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Pass\BuildStopwatch;
use App\Service\Trending\StoryLinkSource\HeadlineLinks;

/** One window grouped in memory, every group and the near misses: tuning without writing a row (#2017). */
final readonly class TrendingExplainer
{
    public function __construct(private TrendingGrouping $grouping, private HeadlineLinks $headlineLinks)
    {
    }

    public function explain(TrendingWindow $window): TrendingExplanationModel
    {
        $stopwatch = BuildStopwatch::start();
        $run = $this->grouping->collect($window);
        $groups = $this->grouping->groups($run);
        usort(
            $groups,
            static fn (TrendingStoryModel $left, TrendingStoryModel $right): int
                => [$right->outletCount(), $right->lastSeen] <=> [$left->outletCount(), $left->lastSeen],
        );
        $nearMisses = $this->headlineLinks->nearMisses($run);

        return new TrendingExplanationModel(
            $window,
            \count($run->entryIds()),
            $groups,
            $nearMisses,
            $stopwatch->elapsedMilliseconds(),
            $stopwatch->peakMemoryBytes(),
        );
    }
}
```

`backend/src/Command/TrendingExplainCommand.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Command;

use App\Entity\Entry;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Exception\ValidationException;
use App\Repository\EntryRepository;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\StoryMemberModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingExplanationModel;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingExplainer;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Attribute\AsCommand;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Command\Command;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Input\InputInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Input\InputOption;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Output\OutputInterface;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Style\SymfonyStyle;

#[AsCommand(
    name: 'app:trending:explain',
    description: 'Group one Trending window in memory and print every group and the near misses. Writes nothing.',
)]
final class TrendingExplainCommand extends Command
{
    private const int NEAR_MISSES_SHOWN = 100;
    private const int TITLE_CHUNK = 500;
    private const int MEGABYTE = 1_048_576;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly TrendingExplainer $explainer,
        private readonly EntryRepository $entries,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    protected function configure(): void
    {
        $this->addOption('window', null, InputOption::VALUE_REQUIRED, 'day or week', TrendingWindow::Day->value);
    }

    protected function execute(InputInterface $input, OutputInterface $output): int
    {
        $io = new SymfonyStyle($input, $output);
        try {
            $window = TrendingWindow::fromRequestValue(ConsoleOption::text($input, 'window'));
        } catch (ValidationException $exception) {
            $io->error($exception->errors['window'] ?? [$exception->getMessage()]);

            return Command::FAILURE;
        }
        $explanation = $this->explainer->explain($window);
        $headlines = $this->headlinesOf($explanation);
        $io->title(self::summaryOf($explanation));
        $this->printGroups($io, $explanation, $headlines);
        $this->printNearMisses($io, $explanation, $headlines);

        return Command::SUCCESS;
    }

    /** @param array<int, string> $headlines */
    private function printGroups(SymfonyStyle $io, TrendingExplanationModel $explanation, array $headlines): void
    {
        foreach ($explanation->groups as $group) {
            $io->section(\sprintf('%d outlets, %d entries', $group->outletCount(), \count($group->members)));
            $io->listing(array_map(
                static fn (StoryMemberModel $member): string => $headlines[$member->entryId] ?? '#' . $member->entryId,
                $group->members,
            ));
        }
    }

    /** @param array<int, string> $headlines */
    private function printNearMisses(SymfonyStyle $io, TrendingExplanationModel $explanation, array $headlines): void
    {
        $shown = \array_slice($explanation->nearMisses, 0, self::NEAR_MISSES_SHOWN);
        $io->section(\sprintf('Near misses: %d of %d', \count($shown), \count($explanation->nearMisses)));
        foreach ($shown as $nearMiss) {
            $io->writeln(\sprintf(
                '%s%s    %s%s    %s',
                $nearMiss->failedRule,
                \PHP_EOL,
                $headlines[$nearMiss->overlap->earlierEntryId] ?? '',
                \PHP_EOL,
                $headlines[$nearMiss->overlap->entryId] ?? '',
            ));
        }
    }

    /** @return array<int, string> "[feed] headline" by entry id, for every entry the explanation prints */
    private function headlinesOf(TrendingExplanationModel $explanation): array
    {
        $headlines = [];
        foreach (array_chunk(self::printedEntryIds($explanation), self::TITLE_CHUNK) as $entryIds) {
            foreach ($this->entries->findAllByIds($entryIds) as $entry) {
                $headlines[$entry->requireId()] = self::headlineOf($entry);
            }
        }

        return $headlines;
    }

    /** @return list<int> */
    private static function printedEntryIds(TrendingExplanationModel $explanation): array
    {
        $entryIds = [];
        foreach ($explanation->groups as $group) {
            foreach ($group->members as $member) {
                $entryIds[] = $member->entryId;
            }
        }
        foreach (\array_slice($explanation->nearMisses, 0, self::NEAR_MISSES_SHOWN) as $nearMiss) {
            array_push($entryIds, $nearMiss->overlap->earlierEntryId, $nearMiss->overlap->entryId);
        }

        return array_values(array_unique($entryIds));
    }

    private static function headlineOf(Entry $entry): string
    {
        return \sprintf('[%s] %s', $entry->getFeed()->getTitle() ?? $entry->getFeed()->getUrl(), $entry->getTitle());
    }

    private static function summaryOf(TrendingExplanationModel $explanation): string
    {
        return \sprintf(
            'Trending %s: %d entries, %d groups, %d ms, peak %.1f MB',
            $explanation->window->value,
            $explanation->entryCount,
            \count($explanation->groups),
            $explanation->durationMilliseconds,
            $explanation->peakMemoryBytes / self::MEGABYTE,
        );
    }
}
```

The command class is `final`, not `readonly`, like `SavedSearchRematchCommand` (Symfony's `Command` is not readonly).

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Service\Trending;

use App\Entity\Entry;
use App\Entity\EntryState;
use App\Entity\TrendingSnapshot;
use App\Entity\TrendingStory;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Enum\SocialNetwork;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\EntryListRepository;
use App\Repository\EntryListRow;
use App\Repository\EntryListRowEnricher;
use App\Repository\TrendingStoryRepository;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryCardModel;
use App\Service\Trending\TrendingStories;
use App\Tests\DbTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingFixtures;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingWiring;

final class TrendingStoriesTest extends DbTestCase
{
    private TrendingFixtures $fixtures;
    private User $reader;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager);
        $this->reader = $this->user('trending-reader@example.com');
    }

    public function testAStoryShowsOneArticlePerFeedLedByTheNewestWithAPicture(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta, $gamma] = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta', 'gamma');
        $social = $this->fixtures->feed('social', SocialNetwork::Bluesky);
        $stranger = $this->fixtures->feed('stranger');
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, $alpha, $beta, $gamma, $social);
        $alphaOld = $this->fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Alpha old', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $alphaNew = $this->fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Alpha new', '2031-05-14 10:00:00');
        $betaPictured = $this->fixtures->entry($beta, 'Beta', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $betaPictured->getImage()->storeUnverified('https://img.example.com/beta.jpg', 800, 450);
        $gammaNewest = $this->fixtures->entry($gamma, 'Gamma', '2031-05-14 11:00:00');
        $post = $this->fixtures->entry($social, 'Post', '2031-05-14 07:00:00');
        $unsubscribed = $this->fixtures->entry($stranger, 'Stranger', '2031-05-14 12:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->story(
            TrendingWindow::Day,
            '2031-05-14 12:00:00',
            $alphaOld,
            $alphaNew,
            $betaPictured,
            $gammaNewest,
            $post,
            $unsubscribed,
        );

        $stories = $this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day);

        self::assertCount(1, $stories);
        self::assertSame($betaPictured->requireId(), $stories[0]->lead->entry->requireId());
        self::assertSame([$gammaNewest->requireId(), $alphaNew->requireId()], self::idsOf($stories[0]->others));
        self::assertSame(4, $stories[0]->outletCount, 'the social account counts, the unsubscribed feed does not');
        self::assertFalse($stories[0]->viewed);
    }

    public function testWithoutAPictureTheNewestArticleLeads(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta, $gamma] = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta', 'gamma');
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, $alpha, $beta, $gamma);
        $older = $this->fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Older', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $newest = $this->fixtures->entry($beta, 'Newest', '2031-05-14 10:00:00');
        $middle = $this->fixtures->entry($gamma, 'Middle', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->story(TrendingWindow::Day, '2031-05-14 10:00:00', $older, $newest, $middle);

        $stories = $this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day);

        self::assertSame($newest->requireId(), $stories[0]->lead->entry->requireId());
        self::assertSame([$middle->requireId(), $older->requireId()], self::idsOf($stories[0]->others));
    }

    public function testTheDayNeedsThreeOfTheReadersFeedsAndRanksWidestThenNewest(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta, $gamma] = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta', 'gamma');
        $social = $this->fixtures->feed('social', SocialNetwork::Bluesky);
        $stranger = $this->fixtures->feed('stranger');
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, $alpha, $beta, $gamma, $social);
        $four = $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 09:00:00', [$alpha, $beta, $gamma, $social]);
        $threeOlder = $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 10:00:00', [$alpha, $beta, $social]);
        $threeNewer = $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 11:00:00', [$alpha, $gamma, $social]);
        $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 12:00:00', [$alpha, $beta, $stranger]);

        $stories = $this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day);

        self::assertSame(
            [$four->requireId(), $threeNewer->requireId(), $threeOlder->requireId()],
            array_map(fn (TrendingStoryCardModel $card): int => $this->storyIdOf($card), $stories),
        );
        self::assertSame([4, 3, 3], array_map(
            static fn (TrendingStoryCardModel $card): int => $card->outletCount,
            $stories,
        ));
    }

    public function testAStoryWithoutAnArticleTheReaderCanSeeIsLeftOut(): void
    {
        $socials = [
            $this->fixtures->feed('ann', SocialNetwork::Bluesky),
            $this->fixtures->feed('ben', SocialNetwork::Mastodon),
            $this->fixtures->feed('cat', SocialNetwork::Bluesky),
        ];
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, ...$socials);
        $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 10:00:00', $socials);

        self::assertSame([], $this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day));
    }

    public function testAStoryIsViewedOnceTheReaderOpenedAnyOfItsEntriesASocialOneIncluded(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta] = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta');
        $social = $this->fixtures->feed('social', SocialNetwork::Bluesky);
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, $alpha, $beta, $social);
        $post = $this->fixtures->entry($social, 'Post', '2031-05-14 07:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->story(
            TrendingWindow::Day,
            '2031-05-14 10:00:00',
            $this->fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Alpha', '2031-05-14 08:00:00'),
            $this->fixtures->entry($beta, 'Beta', '2031-05-14 09:00:00'),
            $post,
        );
        $this->markViewed($this->reader, $post);

        self::assertTrue($this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day)[0]->viewed);
    }

    public function testAnotherReadersViewDoesNotCount(): void
    {
        [$alpha, $beta, $gamma] = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta', 'gamma');
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, $alpha, $beta, $gamma);
        $opened = $this->fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Alpha', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $this->fixtures->story(
            TrendingWindow::Day,
            '2031-05-14 10:00:00',
            $opened,
            $this->fixtures->entry($beta, 'Beta', '2031-05-14 09:00:00'),
            $this->fixtures->entry($gamma, 'Gamma', '2031-05-14 09:30:00'),
        );
        $this->markViewed($this->user('someone-else@example.com'), $opened);

        self::assertFalse($this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Day)[0]->viewed);
    }

    public function testTheWeekNeedsFiveOfTheReadersFeedsAndShowsNoDayStory(): void
    {
        $feeds = $this->articleFeeds('alpha', 'beta', 'gamma', 'delta', 'omega');
        $this->fixtures->subscribe($this->reader, ...$feeds);
        $five = $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 10:00:00', $feeds, TrendingWindow::Week);
        $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 11:00:00', \array_slice($feeds, 0, 4), TrendingWindow::Week);
        $this->storyOf('2031-05-14 12:00:00', $feeds);

        $stories = $this->storiesFor(TrendingWindow::Week);

        self::assertSame([$five->requireId()], array_map(
            fn (TrendingStoryCardModel $card): int => $this->storyIdOf($card),
            $stories,
        ));
    }

    public function testThePageCarriesTheWindowAndWhenItsSnapshotWasBuilt(): void
    {
        $this->entityManager->persist(new TrendingSnapshot(
            TrendingWindow::Day,
            new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 12:00:00'),
            10,
            0,
            5,
            1_000,
        ));
        $this->entityManager->flush();

        $day = $this->stories()->forReader($this->reader->requireId(), TrendingWindow::Day);
        $week = $this->stories()->forReader($this->reader->requireId(), TrendingWindow::Week);

        self::assertSame(TrendingWindow::Day, $day->window);
        self::assertSame('2031-05-14T12:00:00+00:00', $day->builtAt?->format(\DateTimeInterface::ATOM));
        self::assertNull($week->builtAt);
    }

    /** @return list<TrendingStoryCardModel> */
    private function storiesFor(TrendingWindow $window): array
    {
        return $this->stories()->forReader($this->reader->requireId(), $window)->stories;
    }

    private function stories(): TrendingStories
    {
        $storyRepository = $this->entityManager->getRepository(TrendingStory::class);
        self::assertInstanceOf(TrendingStoryRepository::class, $storyRepository);
        $entryList = self::getContainer()->get(EntryListRepository::class);
        self::assertInstanceOf(EntryListRepository::class, $entryList);
        $enricher = self::getContainer()->get(EntryListRowEnricher::class);
        self::assertInstanceOf(EntryListRowEnricher::class, $enricher);

        return new TrendingStories(
            $storyRepository,
            TrendingWiring::snapshots($this->entityManager),
            $entryList,
            $enricher,
        );
    }

    /**
     * One article or post per feed, each published at the story's last-seen time minus its position in hours.
     *
     * @param list<\App\Entity\Feed> $feeds
     */
    private function storyOf(string $lastSeen, array $feeds, TrendingWindow $window = TrendingWindow::Day): TrendingStory
    {
        $members = [];
        foreach ($feeds as $index => $feed) {
            $published = (new \DateTimeImmutable($lastSeen))->modify(\sprintf('-%d hours', $index));
            $members[] = $this->fixtures->entry($feed, 'Story entry', $published->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'));
        }

        return $this->fixtures->story($window, $lastSeen, ...$members);
    }

    /** @return list<\App\Entity\Feed> */
    private function articleFeeds(string ...$names): array
    {
        return array_values(array_map(fn (string $name) => $this->fixtures->feed($name), $names));
    }

    private function storyIdOf(TrendingStoryCardModel $card): int
    {
        $storyId = $this->entityManager->getConnection()->fetchOne(
            'SELECT story_id FROM trending_story_entry WHERE entry_id = ?',
            [$card->lead->entry->requireId()],
        );

        return (int) $storyId;
    }

    private function user(string $email): User
    {
        $user = new User($email, new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-01-01 00:00:00'));
        $this->entityManager->persist($user);
        $this->entityManager->flush();

        return $user;
    }

    private function markViewed(User $user, Entry $entry): void
    {
        $state = new EntryState($user, $entry);
        $state->markViewed(new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 11:00:00'));
        $this->entityManager->persist($state);
        $this->entityManager->flush();
    }

    /**
     * @param list<EntryListRow> $rows
     *
     * @return list<int>
     */
    private static function idsOf(array $rows): array
    {
        return array_map(static fn (EntryListRow $row): int => $row->entry->requireId(), $rows);
    }
}
```

Import `App\Entity\Feed` instead of the fully qualified names in the docblocks; `storyOf()`'s third, defaulted
parameter keeps it at three. Which change each value catches: four subscribed feeds (the social one included) and
the unsubscribed one excluded pin the per-reader count; the picture sits on an *older* entry, so "lead = newest"
fails the first test and "lead = first with picture regardless of order" passes only with newest-first rows; two
alpha entries prove one-per-feed keeps the newer; 3/2 subscribed feeds straddle the day threshold and 5/4 the week's;
equal outlet counts ordered by `last_seen` (11:00 before 10:00); a 4-outlet story at 09:00 ahead of both pins
outlets-before-recency; the second reader's view proves the `user_id` join; the social-only story proves the
displayable-article `EXISTS`.

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Controller\Api;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Tests\Support\ApiTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingFixtures;
use Symfony\Bundle\FrameworkBundle\KernelBrowser;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

final class TrendingControllerTest extends ApiTestCase
{
    private KernelBrowser $client;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->client = self::createClient();
    }

    public function testListsTheReadersStoriesOfTheWindow(): void
    {
        [$headers, $user] = $this->bearerAndUser('trending-api@example.com');
        $fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager());
        $alpha = $fixtures->feed('alpha');
        $beta = $fixtures->feed('beta');
        $gamma = $fixtures->feed('gamma');
        $fixtures->subscribe($user, $alpha, $beta, $gamma);
        $lead = $fixtures->entry($alpha, 'Lead', '2031-05-14 10:00:00');
        $second = $fixtures->entry($beta, 'Second', '2031-05-14 09:00:00');
        $third = $fixtures->entry($gamma, 'Third', '2031-05-14 08:00:00');
        $fixtures->story(TrendingWindow::Day, '2031-05-14 10:00:00', $lead, $second, $third);

        $this->client->request('GET', '/api/trending?window=day', server: $headers);

        self::assertResponseIsSuccessful();
        $payload = $this->payload($this->client);
        self::assertSame('day', $payload['window']);
        self::assertNull($payload['builtAt']);
        self::assertIsArray($payload['stories']);
        self::assertCount(1, $payload['stories']);
        $story = $payload['stories'][0];
        self::assertIsArray($story);
        self::assertSame(['lead', 'others', 'outletCount', 'viewed'], array_keys($story));
        self::assertIsArray($story['lead']);
        self::assertSame($lead->requireId(), $story['lead']['id']);
        self::assertSame('Lead', $story['lead']['title']);
        self::assertIsArray($story['others']);
        self::assertSame(
            [$second->requireId(), $third->requireId()],
            array_map(static fn (mixed $other): mixed => \is_array($other) ? $other['id'] : null, $story['others']),
        );
        self::assertSame(3, $story['outletCount']);
        self::assertFalse($story['viewed']);
    }

    public function testAMissingWindowMeansTheDay(): void
    {
        [$headers] = $this->bearerAndUser('trending-default@example.com');

        $this->client->request('GET', '/api/trending', server: $headers);

        self::assertResponseIsSuccessful();
        self::assertSame(['window' => 'day', 'builtAt' => null, 'stories' => []], $this->payload($this->client));
    }

    public function testAnUnknownWindowIsAValidationProblem(): void
    {
        [$headers] = $this->bearerAndUser('trending-unknown@example.com');

        $this->client->request('GET', '/api/trending?window=month', server: $headers);

        $this->assertRejected($this->client, Response::HTTP_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);
        $errors = $this->payload($this->client)['errors'] ?? null;
        self::assertIsArray($errors);
        self::assertArrayHasKey('window', $errors);
    }

    public function testRejectsAnonymous(): void
    {
        $this->client->request('GET', '/api/trending?window=day');

        self::assertResponseStatusCodeSame(Response::HTTP_UNAUTHORIZED);
    }
}
```

```php
final class TrendingStoryRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    /** A story the reader can see holds at least one entry of a non-social feed they follow. */
    private const string HOLDS_A_SHOWN_ARTICLE = 'SELECT 1 FROM trending_story_entry shown'
        . ' JOIN feed article_feed ON article_feed.id = shown.feed_id AND article_feed.network IS NULL'
        . ' JOIN subscription reader ON reader.feed_id = shown.feed_id AND reader.user_id = :user'
        . ' WHERE shown.story_id = story.id';

    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, TrendingStory::class);
    }

    /**
     * The window's stories at least its threshold of the reader's feeds took part in (social ones count), holding an
     * article the reader can see: widest first, then newest.
     *
     * @return list<RankedStoryModel>
     */
    public function rankedForUser(int $userId, TrendingWindow $window, int $limit): array
    {
        /** @var list<array{story_id: int|string, outlet_count: int|string}> $rows */
        $rows = $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->fetchAllAssociative(
            \sprintf(
                'SELECT story.id AS story_id, COUNT(DISTINCT story_member.feed_id) AS outlet_count'
                . ' FROM trending_story story'
                . ' JOIN trending_story_entry story_member ON story_member.story_id = story.id'
                . ' JOIN subscription subscribed'
                . ' ON subscribed.feed_id = story_member.feed_id AND subscribed.user_id = :user'
                . ' WHERE story.time_window = :window AND EXISTS (%s)'
                . ' GROUP BY story.id, story.last_seen'
                . ' HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT story_member.feed_id) >= :minimum'
                . ' ORDER BY outlet_count DESC, story.last_seen DESC, story.id DESC'
                . ' LIMIT %d',
                self::HOLDS_A_SHOWN_ARTICLE,
                $limit,
            ),
            ['user' => $userId, 'window' => $window->value, 'minimum' => $window->minSubscribedFeeds()],
        );

        return array_map(
            static fn (array $row): RankedStoryModel => new RankedStoryModel(
                (int) $row['story_id'],
                (int) $row['outlet_count'],
            ),
            $rows,
        );
    }

    /**
     * @param list<int> $storyIds
     *
     * @return array<int, list<int>> story id => its entries of the non-social feeds the reader follows
     */
    public function articleEntryIdsByStory(int $userId, array $storyIds): array
    {
        if ($storyIds === []) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var list<array{story_id: int|string, entry_id: int|string}> $rows */
        $rows = $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->fetchAllAssociative(
            'SELECT shown.story_id, shown.entry_id FROM trending_story_entry shown'
            . ' JOIN feed article_feed ON article_feed.id = shown.feed_id AND article_feed.network IS NULL'
            . ' JOIN subscription reader ON reader.feed_id = shown.feed_id AND reader.user_id = :user'
            . ' WHERE shown.story_id IN (:storyIds)',
            ['user' => $userId, 'storyIds' => $storyIds],
            ['storyIds' => ArrayParameterType::INTEGER],
        );

        $entryIds = [];
        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            $entryIds[(int) $row['story_id']][] = (int) $row['entry_id'];
        }

        return $entryIds;
    }

    /**
     * @param list<int> $storyIds
     *
     * @return list<int> the stories one of whose entries, social ones included, the reader opened
     */
    public function viewedStoryIds(int $userId, array $storyIds): array
    {
        if ($storyIds === []) {
            return [];
        }

        /** @var list<int|string> $viewed */
        $viewed = $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->fetchFirstColumn(
            'SELECT DISTINCT opened.story_id FROM trending_story_entry opened'
            . ' JOIN entry_state reading ON reading.entry_id = opened.entry_id'
            . ' AND reading.user_id = :user AND reading.is_viewed = 1'
            . ' WHERE opened.story_id IN (:storyIds)',
            ['user' => $userId, 'storyIds' => $storyIds],
            ['storyIds' => ArrayParameterType::INTEGER],
        );

        return array_map(static fn (int|string $storyId): int => (int) $storyId, $viewed);
    }
}
```

`story_member`, not `member`: `MEMBER` is a reserved word in MySQL 8.0.17+. `:user` appears twice in
`rankedForUser()` (main query and `EXISTS`). Executor: verify DBAL expands a repeated named parameter on both
platforms (it does in DBAL 3/4 to our knowledge); if not, name the second one `:existsUser`.

`backend/src/Service/Trending/Model/TrendingStoryCardModel.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending\Model;

use App\Repository\EntryListRow;

/** One Trending card: an article per feed, led by the newest with a picture (#2017). */
final readonly class TrendingStoryCardModel
{
    /** @param list<EntryListRow> $others newest first */
    public function __construct(
        public EntryListRow $lead,
        public array $others,
        public int $outletCount,
        public bool $viewed,
    ) {
    }

    /** @param non-empty-list<EntryListRow> $newestFirst the story's article rows the reader can see */
    public static function fromRows(array $newestFirst, int $outletCount, bool $viewed): self
    {
        $onePerFeed = self::newestPerFeed($newestFirst);
        $lead = self::leadOf($onePerFeed);
        $others = array_values(array_filter($onePerFeed, static fn (EntryListRow $row): bool => $row !== $lead));

        return new self($lead, $others, $outletCount, $viewed);
    }

    /**
     * @param non-empty-list<EntryListRow> $newestFirst
     *
     * @return non-empty-list<EntryListRow>
     */
    private static function newestPerFeed(array $newestFirst): array
    {
        $byFeed = [];
        foreach ($newestFirst as $row) {
            $byFeed[$row->entry->getFeed()->requireId()] ??= $row;
        }

        return array_values($byFeed);
    }

    /** @param non-empty-list<EntryListRow> $rows */
    private static function leadOf(array $rows): EntryListRow
    {
        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            if ($row->entry->getImageUrl() !== null) {
                return $row;
            }
        }

        return $rows[0];
    }
}
```

`backend/src/Service/Trending/TrendingStories.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Service\Trending;

use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Repository\EntryListRepository;
use App\Repository\EntryListRow;
use App\Repository\EntryListRowEnricher;
use App\Repository\TrendingSnapshotRepository;
use App\Repository\TrendingStoryRepository;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\RankedStoryModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingPageModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryCardModel;

/** One reader's Trending: the shared snapshot cut to their subscriptions and their threshold (#2017). */
final readonly class TrendingStories
{
    public const int MAX_STORIES = 40;

    public function __construct(
        private TrendingStoryRepository $stories,
        private TrendingSnapshotRepository $snapshots,
        private EntryListRepository $entryList,
        private EntryListRowEnricher $enricher,
    ) {
    }

    public function forReader(int $userId, TrendingWindow $window): TrendingPageModel
    {
        $ranked = $this->stories->rankedForUser($userId, $window, self::MAX_STORIES);

        return new TrendingPageModel(
            $window,
            $this->snapshots->builtAtByWindow()[$window->value] ?? null,
            $this->cardsOf($userId, $ranked),
        );
    }

    /**
     * @param list<RankedStoryModel> $ranked
     *
     * @return list<TrendingStoryCardModel>
     */
    private function cardsOf(int $userId, array $ranked): array
    {
        $storyIds = self::storyIdsOf($ranked);
        $entryIdsByStory = $this->stories->articleEntryIdsByStory($userId, $storyIds);
        $rowsByEntryId = $this->rowsByEntryId($userId, array_merge([], ...array_values($entryIdsByStory)));
        $viewedStoryIds = array_flip($this->stories->viewedStoryIds($userId, $storyIds));
        $cards = [];
        foreach ($ranked as $story) {
            $rows = array_values(array_intersect_key(
                $rowsByEntryId,
                array_flip($entryIdsByStory[$story->storyId] ?? []),
            ));
            if ($rows !== []) {
                $cards[] = TrendingStoryCardModel::fromRows($rows, $story->outletCount, isset($viewedStoryIds[$story->storyId]));
            }
        }

        return $cards;
    }

    /**
     * @param list<int> $entryIds
     *
     * @return array<int, EntryListRow> newest first
     */
    private function rowsByEntryId(int $userId, array $entryIds): array
    {
        $rowsByEntryId = [];
        foreach ($this->enricher->enrich($this->entryList->rowsByIdsForUser($userId, $entryIds), $userId) as $row) {
            $rowsByEntryId[$row->entry->requireId()] = $row;
        }

        return $rowsByEntryId;
    }

    /**
     * @param list<RankedStoryModel> $ranked
     *
     * @return list<int>
     */
    private static function storyIdsOf(array $ranked): array
    {
        return array_map(static fn (RankedStoryModel $story): int => $story->storyId, $ranked);
    }
}
```

`array_intersect_key()` keeps `$rowsByEntryId`'s order, so each story's rows stay newest first. A story whose rows
all vanish (an entry purged since the rebuild) is skipped rather than shown empty. Executor: wrap the
`TrendingStoryCardModel::fromRows(...)` line; if PHPMD flags `cardsOf()`'s length or complexity, move the
`foreach` body into a private `cardOf()` taking a small `Pass/` holder for the three lookups (do not add a
fourth parameter).

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Http;

use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingPageModel;
use App\Service\Trending\Model\TrendingStoryCardModel;

final readonly class TrendingJson
{
    public function __construct(private EntryJson $entryJson)
    {
    }

    /**
     * @return array{
     *   window: string,
     *   builtAt: string|null,
     *   stories: list<array{
     *     lead: array<string, mixed>, others: list<array<string, mixed>>, outletCount: int, viewed: bool
     *   }>
     * }
     */
    public function page(TrendingPageModel $page): array
    {
        return [
            'window' => $page->window->value,
            'builtAt' => $page->builtAt?->format(\DateTimeInterface::ATOM),
            'stories' => array_map($this->story(...), $page->stories),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @return array{lead: array<string, mixed>, others: list<array<string, mixed>>, outletCount: int, viewed: bool}
     */
    private function story(TrendingStoryCardModel $story): array
    {
        return [
            'lead' => $this->entryJson->listRow($story->lead),
            'others' => array_map($this->entryJson->listRow(...), $story->others),
            'outletCount' => $story->outletCount,
            'viewed' => $story->viewed,
        ];
    }
}
```

```php
        private TrendingJson $trendingJson,
    ) {
    }
```

### Task 9: The sidebar carries `trending: { day, week }` on both the list and the counts poll

Decisions: D8, D25.

> **Merge-review amendment (2026-11-04).** The first draft added `trending` to `/api/subscriptions/counts` only.
> The frontend fills the badge from `GET /api/subscriptions` at boot and from the counts poll afterwards, so the
> badge would read 0 for up to five minutes. `trending` therefore rides on the shared tallies: it goes into
> `SubscriptionTalliesModel`, and `SubscriptionCountsJson::surfaceTotals()` emits it, which both responses
> spread. No mapper or controller signature changes. `Service/Subscription` gains a dependency on
> `Service/Trending`; Trending imports nothing from Subscription, so `ServiceModuleCycleRule` stays green.

`backend/tests/Controller/Api/SubscriptionTrendingCountsTest.php`:

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Tests\Controller\Api;

use App\Entity\Entry;
use App\Entity\EntryState;
use App\Entity\Feed;
use App\Entity\User;
use App\Enum\TrendingWindow;
use App\Tests\Support\ApiTestCase;
use App\Tests\Support\TrendingFixtures;

final class SubscriptionTrendingCountsTest extends ApiTestCase
{
    public function testTheCountsPollCarriesTheUnopenedTrendingStoriesPerWindow(): void
    {
        $client = self::createClient();
        [$headers, $user] = $this->bearerAndUser('trending-counts@example.com');
        $this->seedStories($user);

        $client->request('GET', '/api/subscriptions/counts', server: $headers);

        self::assertResponseIsSuccessful();
        self::assertSame(['day' => 2, 'week' => 1], $this->payload($client)['trending']);
    }

    public function testTheSidebarListCarriesTheSameCounts(): void
    {
        $client = self::createClient();
        [$headers, $user] = $this->bearerAndUser('trending-list@example.com');
        $this->seedStories($user);

        $client->request('GET', '/api/subscriptions', server: $headers);

        self::assertResponseIsSuccessful();
        self::assertSame(['day' => 2, 'week' => 1], $this->payload($client)['trending']);
    }

    private function seedStories(User $user): void
    {
        $fixtures = new TrendingFixtures($this->entityManager());
        $feeds = array_map(
            static fn (string $name): Feed => $fixtures->feed($name),
            ['alpha', 'beta', 'gamma', 'delta', 'omega'],
        );
        $fixtures->subscribe($user, ...$feeds);

        $this->story($fixtures, TrendingWindow::Day, \array_slice($feeds, 0, 3));
        $this->story($fixtures, TrendingWindow::Day, \array_slice($feeds, 1, 3));
        $opened = $this->story($fixtures, TrendingWindow::Day, \array_slice($feeds, 2, 3));
        $this->story($fixtures, TrendingWindow::Day, \array_slice($feeds, 0, 2));
        $this->story($fixtures, TrendingWindow::Week, $feeds);
        $this->markViewed($user, $opened);
    }

    /**
     * @param list<Feed> $feeds
     *
     * @return Entry the story's first entry
     */
    private function story(TrendingFixtures $fixtures, TrendingWindow $window, array $feeds): Entry
    {
        $members = array_map(
            static fn (Feed $feed): Entry => $fixtures->entry($feed, 'Story', '2031-05-14 09:00:00'),
            $feeds,
        );
        $fixtures->story($window, '2031-05-14 10:00:00', ...$members);

        return $members[0];
    }

    private function markViewed(User $user, Entry $entry): void
    {
        $state = new EntryState($user, $entry);
        $state->markViewed(new \DateTimeImmutable('2031-05-14 11:00:00'));
        $this->entityManager()->persist($state);
        $this->entityManager()->flush();
    }
}
```

What each value catches: there are three day stories over the threshold and one of them is opened, so the
answer is `2`. Counting viewed stories gives 3, and counting the 2-feed story gives 3 or 4. The week story with
all five feeds gives `1`, because the three-feed day stories are under the week's 5, so a shared threshold would
change it. `day` ≠ `week` catches swapped keys. The second test catches `trending` missing from the list
endpoint, the gap this amendment closes. Executor: verify `TrendingFixtures`' method names against Task 5 and
`markViewed()` against `EntryState`.

```php
    /** The stories forReader() would list, minus the opened ones: the badge never counts a card the list lacks. */
    public function unopenedCounts(int $userId): TrendingCountsModel
```

### Task 10: Tuning and acceptance on the dev database

Decision: D32 (with D5's fallback if the week does not fit).

No TDD here. **The dev database (`feedreader` in the Docker MySQL) is read-only for this task**: never migrate it,
never run a test against it, never run the builder, `rebuildDue()`, the worker message or `/maintenance/tick` against
it. `app:trending:explain` writes nothing and needs no Trending table, which is why it is the instrument.

  `docker compose up`): `docker compose exec php git -C /app log -1 --format=%h 2>/dev/null || true`
  (Executor: use whatever path the php service mounts the backend at; the point is that the code inside the container
  is this branch's). Then `docker compose exec php bin/console cache:clear` so the new command is registered.
