- [ ] **Step 3: Explain both windows and keep the output.**

```bash
docker compose exec php php -d memory_limit=512M bin/console app:trending:explain --window=day > /tmp/trending-day.txt
docker compose exec php php -d memory_limit=512M bin/console app:trending:explain --window=week > /tmp/trending-week.txt
head -1 /tmp/trending-day.txt; head -1 /tmp/trending-week.txt
```

The title line reads `Trending week: N entries, G groups, D ms, peak M MB`. The explain also computes near misses,
so its time and memory are an upper bound of the build's.

- [ ] **Step 4: Acceptance on numbers.** The week line must show **< 30 000 ms and < 128 MB**. If it does not:
  1. profile (`php -d xdebug.mode=profile` or `SPX` if installed) and look first at
     `HeadlineWindow::sharedWeightsWithEarlier()` / `earlierHoldersOf()` (the per-pair allocations) — replacing the
     `array_fill_keys(...) + array_filter(...)` pair with one loop is the first fix to try;
  2. only if that is not enough, apply D5's fallback (build the week alone and derive the day from it) — that is a
     design change: stop and ask the user before doing it.

- [ ] **Step 5: Acceptance on quality.** Read the first 20 groups of each file (they are sorted widest first). For
  every group, check that all headlines tell one event. Record each wrong merge with its headlines. Then read the
  near misses (`Near misses: …` section) for missed links that should have formed.

- [ ] **Step 6: Tune only `TrendingWindow` and the two `HeadlineLinks` / `HeadlineFiller` constants**, one change at
  a time, rerunning Step 3 after each and noting the effect (group count, the wrong merge gone or not). Every changed
  number needs its tests updated so a value still sits on each side of the new threshold (Tasks 1–3 name which test
  pins which constant). Do not add a rule without asking: a new rule is a design change.

- [ ] **Step 7: Record for the PR**: both windows' entry counts, group counts, `ms` and `MB` lines; the 20+20 reviewed
  groups' verdict ("no wrong merge" or the list and what was tuned); any constant changed with before/after numbers.
  Paste into the PR body, not into a file in the repo.

- [ ] **Step 8: Gates and both legs again** if any constant changed (Step 6), then commit
  `fix(#2017): tune trending thresholds against the dev database` (skip the commit if nothing changed).

- [ ] **Step 9: Leave the dev database migration to the user.** Do **not** run
  `doctrine:migrations:migrate` against the Docker stack; say in the hand-off that the stack needs
  `docker compose exec php bin/console doctrine:migrations:migrate --no-interaction` (and a worker restart, so the

### Executor notes (backend)

- `MB_CASE_FOLD` must fold `ß` to `ss` (full case folding); `HeadlineStemsTest` asserts it. If this PHP build does
  simple folding only, the test tells you — then add an explicit `str_replace('ß', 'ss', …)` and say why in one line.
- `hrtime(true)`'s PHPStan type (`int|float|false`?) — the `(int)` casts in `BuildStopwatch` cover all three; tighten
  if PHPStan reports an always-true cast.
- Whether this ORM version accepts a backed enum as an `#[ORM\Id]`: the plan sidesteps it with a string id on
  `TrendingSnapshot` (`getWindow()` converts).
- DBAL repeated named parameters (`:user` twice in `rankedForUser()`): believed supported on both platforms; the
  MySQL leg proves it.
- The test container may inline private services used once (`StoryLinks`, `TrendingEntryRepository`,
  `TrendingStories` before Task 9): tests build them by hand (`TrendingWiring`, `TrendingStoriesTest::stories()`);
  only `TrendingContainerWiringTest` fetches from the container, with a fallback named in its step.
- `EntryListRepository::rowsByIdsForUser()` collapses copies sharing a `url_hash` into the lowest-id survivor's
  `duplicates`; a Trending card can therefore show an outlet inside the lead's "Also in" rather than in `others`. Left
  as is (the frontend half's footer sees both); mention it in the PR.
- The story order's tiebreak uses `trending_story.last_seen` (the instance-wide newest member), not the newest entry
  the reader follows — cheaper and nearly always equal; D24's wording allows either reading. Flag it in the PR.
- `TrendingGrouping::stories()` drops groups below the window's minimum *instance-wide* feeds at build time: such a
  group can never reach any reader's threshold. Not in the spec text; it only saves rows.
- Two departures from the spec's wording (folder `StoryLinkSource/`, 422 through `ValidationException`) are explained
  at the top of Part A; restate them in the PR.
- `MaintenanceTick` now runs the private-feed sync before the membership sweep (so Trending is last, D23). Executor:
  grep `MaintenanceTickTest` for any assertion on that order before the change (none found while planning).
- Performance of `HeadlineWindow` on the 40k-entry week is the main unknown; Task 10 measures it before anything is
  tuned, and D5's fallback needs the user's go-ahead.

---

## Part B — Frontend

**Architecture (frontend).** Trending is one more list in the reader, not a page of its own. The selection
vocabulary gains the kind `'trending'` (`?view=trending`), carrying the window the way a list carries its order:
not in the URL, applied from a per-account device setting (`sfr.user.<userId>.trending-window`) by
`ListPreferences`. `queryFromSelection` turns it into `{ view: 'trending', window }`, and `ReaderApi.entries()`
answers that query from `GET /api/trending` instead of `/api/entries`, adapting each story to its **lead**: an
ordinary `EntryDto` with a client-side `coverage` field (`outletCount`, `others`, `viewed`). So `EntriesStore`
holds the leads and nothing else, and every existing path works on them unchanged: the reload on selection
change, the magazine planner and both card layouts, the open-article route, the optimistic state patches, the
neighbour prefetch, the error banner. A separate Trending store would have duplicated all of that. This is a
deliberate deviation from the task split's "a store/service for the Trending list": the store **is**
`EntriesStore`; Task 11 extends it to patch `coverage.others` exactly as it patches `duplicates`.

The footer generalises per decision 29: `entry-duplicates` takes `copies` and `label`; one pure function,
`alsoFootOf(entry)`, picks duplicates + "Also in" or the story's others + "Also covered by", so the 11 magazine
blocks and `entry-row` bind the same two expressions.

Viewed (decisions 6, 7, 28): a story is viewed when the server said so, or when the lead or any listed other
outlet carries `isViewed`. Opening any of them already runs `EntryStateActions.markOpenedViewed` →
`EntriesStore.setState({ isViewed: true })`, which patches that entry where it sits (lead row or
`coverage.others`), so the story dims at once with no refetch; the success path's `reloadCountsQuietly()` brings
the badge down. Dimming = the existing read-state mark (the unread dot goes off) plus a dimmed card slot
(`--viewed-story-opacity`).

### Global Constraints (frontend)

- Commits `type(#2017): lower-case summary`; no Claude attribution anywhere (no "Generated with", no
  `Co-Authored-By`, no session links).
- CLAUDE.md "Frontend conventions" apply: standalone components and signals; styles in a sibling `.scss` via
  `styleUrl`; no hex colours, ad-hoc `px` spacing or media-query literals in component scss (tokens and
  `bp.$…`; a list-header rule goes in the `list-header` `@container`, interpolated). Read
  `docs/design-language.md` before adding a surface.
- **Offer the architectural fix over a patch** when a frontend bug traces back to a weakness; let Lars choose.
- Never nest `cdkDropList`s. Reader-list popovers go on a CDK overlay (`cdkConnectedOverlay`), never an
  absolutely positioned box inside the list.
- Frontend tests run inside Docker: `docker compose exec -T frontend npm test -- <pattern>`. The gate is
  `npm run check` from `frontend/` (ESLint + Prettier 100 cols + Stylelint + tsc + Jest). `npm run check`
  misses template errors: also run `npm run build` (from `frontend/`, or
  `docker compose exec -T frontend npm run build`).
- Every test value must be able to fail: each task carries a deletion check. Restore **without**
  `git checkout`: copy the file aside first (`cp X X.bak`), break it, run, `mv X.bak X`, run again, paste both
  outputs into the task report.
- Translation keys a task's tests read go into `frontend/public/i18n/en.json` and `de.json` **in that task**
  (the specs load the real dictionaries); Task 15 audits parity.
- **Implementer instruction for every task:** review the plan adversarially before coding — the plan is the
  usual defect source (wrong line, stale signature, a test value that dodges its own branch). Read each named
  file first; fix what is wrong and amend this plan document in-branch in the same commit.
- Depends on Part A's contract only (`GET /api/trending`, `trending` in the counts). Frontend tasks never call
  a real `/api/trending` in Jest; Playwright stubs it.

```ts
export type TrendingWindow = 'day' | 'week';

export type TrendingCounts = Readonly<Record<TrendingWindow, number>>;

/** One story of `GET /api/trending` (#2017): the lead and the other article outlets are list rows. */
export interface TrendingStoryDto {
  lead: EntryDto;
  others: EntryDto[];
  /** Distinct subscribed feeds in the story, social ones included. */
  outletCount: number;
  viewed: boolean;
}

export interface TrendingResponseDto {
  window: TrendingWindow;
  builtAt: string | null;
  stories: TrendingStoryDto[];
}

export interface TrendingCoverage {
  outletCount: number;
  others: EntryDto[];
  /** The server's verdict: some entry of the story, a social one included, was opened. */
  viewed: boolean;
}
```

```ts
import { anEntry } from '../../../testing/entry-testing';

import { trendingPage } from './trending-page';

const response = (): TrendingResponseDto => ({
  window: 'day',

  stories: [
    {
      lead: anEntry({ id: 1, title: 'Lead one' }),
      others: [anEntry({ id: 2 }), anEntry({ id: 3 })],
      outletCount: 7,
      viewed: false,
    },
    { lead: anEntry({ id: 4, title: 'Lead two' }), others: [], outletCount: 3, viewed: true },
  ],
});

describe('trendingPage', () => {
  it('lists each story as its lead, in the order the server ranked them', () => {
    expect(trendingPage(response()).entries.map((entry) => entry.id)).toEqual([1, 4]);
  });

  it('carries the story on its lead: outlet count, other outlets and the viewed verdict', () => {
    const [first, second] = trendingPage(response()).entries;

    expect(first.coverage).toEqual({
      outletCount: 7,
      others: [anEntry({ id: 2 }), anEntry({ id: 3 })],
      viewed: false,
    });
    expect(second.coverage?.viewed).toBe(true);

    expect(trendingPage(response()).entries[0].title).toBe('Lead one');
  });

});
```

```ts
import { WritableSignal, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { provideAccountIdentity } from '../../../testing/account-identity-testing';
import { TrendingWindowService } from './trending-window.service';

describe('TrendingWindowService', () => {
  let userId: WritableSignal<number | null>;
  const service = () => TestBed.inject(TrendingWindowService);

  beforeEach(() => {
    localStorage.clear();
    userId = signal<number | null>(3);
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({ providers: [provideAccountIdentity(userId)] });
  });

  it('starts on the past 24 hours', () => {
    expect(service().window()).toBe('day');
  });

  it("reads the account's stored week back", () => {
    localStorage.setItem('sfr.user.3.trending-window', 'week');
    expect(service().window()).toBe('week');
  });

  it('stores the week under the account and forgets it again for the day', () => {
    service().set('week');
    expect(localStorage.getItem('sfr.user.3.trending-window')).toBe('week');
    expect(service().window()).toBe('week');

    service().set('day');
    expect(localStorage.getItem('sfr.user.3.trending-window')).toBeNull();
    expect(service().window()).toBe('day');
  });

  it("keeps one account's window from the next", () => {
    service().set('week');
    userId.set(4);
    expect(service().window()).toBe('day');
  });

  it('reads a garbage stored value as the day', () => {
    localStorage.setItem('sfr.user.3.trending-window', 'month');
    expect(service().window()).toBe('day');
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/trending/trending-window.ts`:

```ts
import { TrendingCounts, TrendingWindow } from '../models';

export const DEFAULT_TRENDING_WINDOW: TrendingWindow = 'day';

export const NO_TRENDING_COUNTS: TrendingCounts = { day: 0, week: 0 };

export function isTrendingWindow(value: string | null): value is TrendingWindow {
  return value === 'day' || value === 'week';
}

export function sameTrendingCounts(left: TrendingCounts, right: TrendingCounts): boolean {
  return left.day === right.day && left.week === right.week;
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/trending/trending-page.ts`:

```ts
import { EntriesPage, EntryDto, TrendingResponseDto, TrendingStoryDto } from '../models';

/** The Trending list as the reader's list store holds any list: one row per story, its lead (#2017). */
export function trendingPage(response: TrendingResponseDto): EntriesPage {
  return { entries: response.stories.map(leadOf), nextCursor: null };
}

function leadOf(story: TrendingStoryDto): EntryDto {
  return {
    ...story.lead,
    coverage: { outletCount: story.outletCount, others: story.others, viewed: story.viewed },
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/trending/trending-window.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable, computed, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { UserDeviceStorage } from '../../core/preferences/user-device-storage';
import { TrendingWindow } from '../models';
import { DEFAULT_TRENDING_WINDOW, isTrendingWindow } from './trending-window';

const NAME = 'trending-window';

/** Which window the Trending list shows, per account on this device (#2017, decision 9). */
@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class TrendingWindowService {
  private readonly storage = inject(UserDeviceStorage);

  readonly window = computed<TrendingWindow>(() => {
    const stored = this.storage.read(NAME);
    return isTrendingWindow(stored) ? stored : DEFAULT_TRENDING_WINDOW;
  });

  set(window: TrendingWindow): void {
    this.storage.write(NAME, window === DEFAULT_TRENDING_WINDOW ? null : window);
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/state/entries.store.ts`:
- Replace `entryOrCopy` (line ~267) with:

```ts
  /** The entry with `id`, or a copy inside a row — a duplicate, or an outlet of a Trending story — which the
   *  footer popover acts on. */
  private entryOrCopy(id: number): EntryDto | null {
    const listed = this.entry(id);
    if (listed) return listed;
    const held = this.unlisted();
    const rows = held ? [...this.rawEntries(), held] : this.rawEntries();
    return rows.flatMap(copiesOf).find((copy) => copy.id === id) ?? null;
  }
```

- Change `patchEntries`' doc comment to "Reaches each target as a row and as a copy inside a row's footer
  (`duplicates`, `coverage.others`). …" (keep its second sentence).
- Replace `withPatchedRow` (line ~341) with:

```ts
/** `row` itself when neither it nor a copy in its footer is targeted. */
function withPatchedRow(
  row: EntryDto,
  targets: ReadonlySet<number>,
  patch: (entry: EntryDto) => EntryDto,
): EntryDto {
  const patched = targets.has(row.id) ? patch(row) : row;
  return withPatchedCoverage(withPatchedDuplicates(patched, targets, patch), targets, patch);
}

function withPatchedDuplicates(
  row: EntryDto,
  targets: ReadonlySet<number>,
  patch: (entry: EntryDto) => EntryDto,
): EntryDto {
  if (!row.duplicates?.some((copy) => targets.has(copy.id))) return row;
  return {
    ...row,
    duplicates: row.duplicates.map((copy) => (targets.has(copy.id) ? patch(copy) : copy)),
  };
}

function withPatchedCoverage(
  row: EntryDto,
  targets: ReadonlySet<number>,
  patch: (entry: EntryDto) => EntryDto,
): EntryDto {
  const coverage = row.coverage;
  if (!coverage?.others.some((other) => targets.has(other.id))) return row;
  const others = coverage.others.map((other) => (targets.has(other.id) ? patch(other) : other));
  return { ...row, coverage: { ...coverage, others } };
}

function copiesOf(row: EntryDto): EntryDto[] {
  return [...(row.duplicates ?? []), ...(row.coverage?.others ?? [])];
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/list/magazine/also-foot.ts`:

```ts
import { EntryDto } from '../../models';

/** What a card's footer lists, and the translation key it is headed with. */
export interface AlsoFoot {
  readonly copies: readonly EntryDto[];
  readonly label: string;
}

const NO_COPIES: readonly EntryDto[] = [];

export function alsoFootOf(entry: EntryDto): AlsoFoot {
  return { copies: entry.duplicates ?? NO_COPIES, label: 'reader.alsoIn' };
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/list/magazine/entry-duplicates.component.ts` — replace the class body's inputs and
`copies`/`displayed` members (keep providers, positions, the action methods and `close()` as they are):

```ts
/** The footer's chip limit: a capped footer keeps a card's height predictable for the planner (#2017). */
export const ALSO_FOOT_NAMES = 4;

@Component({ /* unchanged */ })
export class EntryDuplicatesComponent implements EntryActionHandler {
  /** The row the footer sits on; deleting from a copy's card deletes this row's post (#1972). */
  readonly entry = input.required<EntryDto>();
  readonly copies = input.required<readonly EntryDto[]>();
  readonly label = input.required<string>();
  private readonly shellActions = inject(EntryActionHandler, { skipSelf: true });

  private readonly language = inject(LanguageService);
  private readonly rows = inject(SubscriptionRows);
  private readonly named = computed(() =>
    this.copies().map((copy) => ({
      copy,
      feed: this.rows.find(copy.subscriptionId)?.title ?? copy.source,
      when: relativeTime(copy.publishedAt ?? copy.createdAt, this.language.lang()),
    })),
  );
  private readonly expanded = signal(false);
  protected readonly shown = computed(() =>
    this.expanded() ? this.named() : this.named().slice(0, ALSO_FOOT_NAMES),
  );
  protected readonly hiddenCount = computed(() => this.named().length - this.shown().length);

  private readonly displayedId = signal<number | null>(null);
  /** The shown copy as the row the store keeps in sync holds it, so an action's optimistic flip and revert show. */
  protected readonly displayed = computed(
    () => this.copies().find((copy) => copy.id === this.displayedId()) ?? null,
  );
  protected readonly origin = signal<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  protected readonly positions = OVERLAY_POSITIONS;
  protected readonly panelId = `dup-popover-${nextId++}`;

  showAll(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.expanded.set(true);
  }

  /* toggle, open, favorite, like, keep, unshare, toggleRead, deletePost, close: unchanged */
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/list/magazine/entry-duplicates.component.html` — the head of the foot becomes:

```html
@if (copies().length) {
  <div class="also-foot">
    <app-icon class="layers" name="layers" size="sm" aria-hidden="true" />
    <span class="lead">{{ label() | transloco }}</span>
    @for (item of shown(); track item.copy.id) {
      <button
        type="button"
        class="also-entry reads-as-text"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="displayed()?.id === item.copy.id"
        [attr.aria-controls]="displayed()?.id === item.copy.id ? panelId : null"
        (click)="toggle(item.copy, $event)"
      >
        {{ item.feed }} <span class="sep">·</span>
        <span class="t">{{ item.when }}</span>
      </button>
    }
    @if (hiddenCount() > 0) {
      <button
        type="button"
        class="also-more reads-as-text"
        (click)="showAll($event)"
        (keydown.enter)="$event.stopPropagation()"
        (keydown.space)="$event.stopPropagation()"
      >
        {{ 'reader.alsoMore' | transloco: { count: hiddenCount() } }}
      </button>
    }
  </div>
```

```ts
export function trendingWindowOf(selection: Selection): TrendingWindow {
  return selection.window ?? DEFAULT_TRENDING_WINDOW;
}

/** Applies the account's window to a Trending selection parsed from the URL, which never carries one. */
export function withTrendingWindow(selection: Selection, window: TrendingWindow): Selection {
  return selection.kind === 'trending' ? { ...selection, window } : selection;
}

/** Trending is a picture of the news, not an inbox: nothing in it is marked read in bulk (#2017). */
export function offersMarkAboveRead(selection: Selection): boolean {
  return selection.kind !== 'trending';
}
```

  `frontend/src/app/reader/list/magazine/also-foot.spec.ts`,

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EntryDto.coverage`, `TrendingWindowService`, `trendingWindowOf`, `offersMarkAboveRead`,
  `alsoFootOf`, `EntryDuplicatesComponent` (Tasks 11–13).
- Produces: `isStoryViewed(entry)`, `showsUnreadMark(entry)`; `<app-trending-window-switch>`; `.sources` in the
  kicker line and the row meta; `.row-slot.viewed-story`; token `--viewed-story-opacity`; i18n
  `reader.alsoCoveredBy`, `reader.trendingSources`, `reader.trendingWindow.label`, `reader.trendingWindow.day`,
  `reader.trendingWindow.week`, `reader.trendingEmptyDay`,
  `reader.trendingEmptyWeek`.

Decisions recorded here:
- **Switch (amended 2026-11-04, Lars: two visible options):** both windows are visible at once, as the shared
  `<app-segmented-choice>` (design-language § `<app-segmented-choice>`) with options `day` / `week`, labels
  `24 h` / `7 days`, group name "Time window". It is rendered through the shell's `headerActions` slot (the list
  header only owns the slot, #581), so `entry-list` and `list-header` gain no input or output. This is the first
  segmented control in a list header: the executor checks it fits the header row height and the narrow
  (phone) header in the live check, and records the placement as an exception in `docs/design-language.md` if
  its height differs from the list actions'.
- **Header:** no unread switch, no order toggle, no Refresh, no mark-all-read and no mark-above-read follow from
  Task 13's predicates; the title pill counts stories.
- **Opening and next/prev:** the leads are the list, so opening a lead is the ordinary open, and the reader's
  neighbour logic (`ReaderRouteState.prefetchNeighbours` over `EntriesStore.entries()`) walks the leads. Opening an
  outlet from the footer popover is the existing duplicates path (an unlisted entry, held by `holdUnlisted`).
- **Planner check:** `BLOCK_HEIGHT` and `pageHeight` never counted the footer — not for duplicates today either —
  and they "need only be right relative to each other" (`magazine-block.ts`). The capped footer adds at most one
  row of four chips (two when it wraps on a phone) to a card, the same for every Trending card, so the relative
  heights and the 1100-unit page cap's job (no three heroes on a page) are unchanged. No planner change; the cap
  itself is pinned by Task 12's tests.

```ts
import { signal } from '@angular/core';
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { provideTranslocoTesting } from '../../../../testing/transloco-testing';
import { provideAccountIdentity } from '../../../../testing/account-identity-testing';
import { TrendingWindowSwitchComponent } from './trending-window-switch.component';

function mount() {

    imports: [TrendingWindowSwitchComponent, provideTranslocoTesting()],
    providers: [provideAccountIdentity(signal(6))],
  });
  const fixture = TestBed.createComponent(TrendingWindowSwitchComponent);
  fixture.detectChanges();
  return fixture;
}

const option = (fixture: ReturnType<typeof mount>, label: string) =>
  [...(fixture.nativeElement as HTMLElement).querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.seg button')].find(
    (button) => button.textContent!.trim() === label,
  )!;

describe('TrendingWindowSwitchComponent', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('shows both windows, the past 24 hours pressed by default', () => {
    const fixture = mount();
    const group = (fixture.nativeElement as HTMLElement).querySelector('.seg')!;

    expect(group.getAttribute('aria-label')).toBe('Time window');
    expect(option(fixture, '24 h').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(option(fixture, '7 days').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
  });

  it('picks the past 7 days and stores it for the account', () => {
    const fixture = mount();

    option(fixture, '7 days').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(option(fixture, '7 days').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(option(fixture, '24 h').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('false');
    expect(localStorage.getItem('sfr.user.6.trending-window')).toBe('week');
  });

  it('picks the day again', () => {
    localStorage.setItem('sfr.user.6.trending-window', 'week');
    const fixture = mount();

    option(fixture, '24 h').click();
    fixture.detectChanges();

    expect(localStorage.getItem('sfr.user.6.trending-window')).toBeNull();
    expect(option(fixture, '24 h').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/trending/coverage.ts`:

```ts
import { EntryDto } from '../models';

/** A Trending story is viewed once any of its entries was opened: by the server's word, which counts
 *  entries the card does not show, or because the lead or a listed outlet was opened here (#2017). */
export function isStoryViewed(entry: EntryDto): boolean {
  const coverage = entry.coverage;
  if (!coverage) return false;
  return coverage.viewed || entry.isViewed || coverage.others.some((other) => other.isViewed);
}

/** Whether a card shows the unread dot: an ordinary row by its read state, a Trending lead by its story. */
export function showsUnreadMark(entry: EntryDto): boolean {
  return entry.coverage ? !isStoryViewed(entry) : !entry.isHidden;
}
```

`frontend/src/app/reader/list/magazine/also-foot.ts` — `alsoFootOf` becomes:

```ts
export function alsoFootOf(entry: EntryDto): AlsoFoot {
  if (entry.coverage) return { copies: entry.coverage.others, label: 'reader.alsoCoveredBy' };
  return { copies: entry.duplicates ?? NO_COPIES, label: 'reader.alsoIn' };
}
```

### Task 15: Translations audit, one Playwright smoke, live check (decisions 30, 36)

**Files:**
- Modify (only if the audit finds a gap): `frontend/public/i18n/en.json`, `frontend/public/i18n/de.json`
- Create: `frontend/e2e/trending-smoke.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; `signInWithLayout` (`e2e/support/auth.ts`), `entryWire` (`e2e/support/reader.ts`).
- Produces: nothing new.

- [ ] **Step 1: Audit the keys**

From `frontend/`:

```bash
node -e "
const en = require('./public/i18n/en.json').reader, de = require('./public/i18n/de.json').reader;
const keys = ['alsoMore','alsoCoveredBy','trending','trendingSources','trendingWindow','trendingEmptyDay',
  'trendingEmptyWeek'];
for (const key of keys) console.log(key, key in en ? 'en' : 'MISSING-en', key in de ? 'de' : 'MISSING-de');
"
```

Expected: every line `… en de`. Fix any `MISSING`. (Executor: if the repo has a dictionary-parity spec —
`grep -rln "de.json" frontend/src --include='*.spec.ts'` — run it too.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the Playwright smoke**

`frontend/e2e/trending-smoke.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, Page, Request, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { signInWithLayout } from './support/auth';
import { entryWire } from './support/reader';
import type { EntryDto, TrendingResponseDto, TrendingWindow } from '../src/app/reader/models';

/**
 * Stubs `GET /api/trending` so the spec owns every story it asserts on (#2017): a fresh database has no
 * snapshot, and the seeded account's real one changes every ten minutes. Read-only — no card is opened,
 * so no entry state on the shared stack moves.
 */
const outlet = (id: number, source: string): EntryDto => entryWire({ id, source, subscriptionId: id });

const DAY: TrendingResponseDto = {
  window: 'day',
  builtAt: '2026-11-04T08:00:00Z',
  stories: [
    {
      lead: entryWire({ id: 9001, title: 'Trending fixture day story', source: 'Fixture lead' }),
      others: [9002, 9003, 9004, 9005, 9006, 9007].map((id) => outlet(id, `Outlet ${id}`)),
      outletCount: 7,
      viewed: false,
    },
    {
      lead: entryWire({ id: 9011, title: 'Trending fixture opened story', source: 'Fixture lead' }),
      others: [outlet(9012, 'Outlet 9012')],
      outletCount: 3,
      viewed: true,
    },
  ],
};

const WEEK: TrendingResponseDto = {
  window: 'week',
  builtAt: '2026-11-04T08:00:00Z',
  stories: [
    {
      lead: entryWire({ id: 9021, title: 'Trending fixture week story', source: 'Fixture lead' }),
      others: [outlet(9022, 'Outlet 9022')],
      outletCount: 5,
      viewed: false,
    },
  ],
};

async function stubTrending(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  await page.route(
    (url) => url.pathname === '/api/trending',
    async (route) => {
      const window = new URL(route.request().url()).searchParams.get('window') as TrendingWindow;
      await route.fulfill({ status: 200, json: window === 'week' ? WEEK : DAY });
    },
  );
}

const trendingRequest = (window: TrendingWindow) => (request: Request) => {
  const url = new URL(request.url());
  return url.pathname === '/api/trending' && url.searchParams.get('window') === window;
};

const slotOf = (page: Page, id: number) =>
  page.locator('.row-slot', { has: page.locator(`[data-entry-id="${id}"]`) });

for (const layout of ['magazine', 'list'] as const) {
  test(`Trending lists stories, caps the footer and switches the window (${layout})`, async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await stubTrending(page);
    test.skip(
      !(await signInWithLayout(page, layout)),
      'seeded admin login unavailable (run app:e2e:seed-admin against the stack)',
    );

    const dayLoaded = page.waitForRequest(trendingRequest('day'));
    await page
      .getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Feeds' })
      .getByRole('link', { name: 'Trending' })
      .click();
    await dayLoaded;

    const story = slotOf(page, 9001);
    await expect(story.getByText('Trending fixture day story')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(story.locator('.sources')).toHaveText('7 sources');
    await expect(story.locator('.also-foot .lead')).toHaveText('Also covered by');
    await expect(story.locator('.also-entry')).toHaveCount(4);

    await story.locator('.also-more').click();
    await expect(story.locator('.also-entry')).toHaveCount(6);
    expect(new URL(page.url()).searchParams.get('entry')).toBeNull();

    await expect(slotOf(page, 9011)).toHaveClass(/viewed-story/);
    await expect(story).not.toHaveClass(/viewed-story/);

    const header = page.locator('app-list-header').first();
    await expect(header.locator('.unread-switch')).toHaveCount(0);
    await expect(header.locator('.mark-all')).toHaveCount(0);

    const weekLoaded = page.waitForRequest(trendingRequest('week'));
    await header.getByRole('button', { name: '7 days' }).click();
    await weekLoaded;
    await expect(page.getByText('Trending fixture week story')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(header.getByRole('button', { name: '7 days' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    await expect(header.getByRole('button', { name: '24 h' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  });
}
```

(Executor: the sidebar link's accessible name gains the badge number when the real backend reports unopened
stories — `getByRole('link', { name: 'Trending' })` matches by substring, so that is fine; see memory
"getByRole matches on substring" if a second link ever contains "Trending". If the list header renders twice in
split view, `.first()` picks the visible list's.)

Needs the Docker stack up, from the checkout that ran `docker compose up` (CLAUDE.md "e2e is black-box").

Expected: 2 passed.

- [ ] **Step 5: Live check on http://localhost:4200** (memory "Live checks on :4200"; verify the container
  serves the new chunk first — memory ":4200 serves a stale chunk")

Needs Part A deployed to the dev stack and a built snapshot (`docker compose exec php bin/console app:trending:explain --window=day`
shows groups; the worker rebuilds every 5 min). Check and note each in the PR:
- [ ] Sidebar: Trending sits right after Recently read and directly above For you; badge = unopened stories of the
      chosen window; switching the window moves the badge; opening a story lowers it after the counts reload.
- [ ] Magazine layout, desktop (≥1280) and phone (375×812): leads render through the normal blocks (hero, wide,
      split…), the kicker reads "N sources" on one line (long source ellipsised, never the count or the time),
      the footer reads "Also covered by" with ≤4 chips and "+N more"; "+N more" reveals the rest in place and

- [ ] List layout, desktop and phone: the same on `entry-row` (count in the meta line, footer below).
- [ ] Window switch: both options "24 h" and "7 days" visible as a segmented control, in the wide header and in

- [ ] Header shows no unread switch, no order toggle, no Refresh, no mark-all-read; no mark-above-read corner
      button after scrolling.
- [ ] Opening a lead opens the article page; back in the list its card is dimmed and its dot is off; the reader's
      neighbour prefetch walks the leads (network panel: `/api/entries/<next lead id>` after opening one).
      Opening an outlet from a footer popover dims its story too.
- [ ] Trending cards beside ordinary duplicates: an All items row with duplicates still reads "Also in", same
      chip look, same cap.
- [ ] Dark mode (both layouts): the dimmed card stays legible; tune `--viewed-story-opacity` per theme if not.
- [ ] Empty window (e.g. week on a quiet dev DB, or stub): the rule sentence shows.
- [ ] Scan today's dev log for errors from `/api/trending` (`ls -t backend/var/log/dev-*.log | head -1 | xargs tail -n 200 | jq .`).

### Executor notes

1. **Counts on the bootstrap response: resolved.** Part A's Task 9 (amended at merge) puts `trending` into
   `SubscriptionCountsJson::surfaceTotals()`, so `GET /api/subscriptions` and `/counts` both carry it. Keep it on
   `SubscriptionsResponse`.
2. **No separate Trending store** (see the architecture note): the list rides on `EntriesStore` through
   `ReaderApi.entries()`. If a reviewer wants `builtAt` shown ("Built 4 min ago" in the header), `EntriesPage`
   would need a field for it; out of scope here.
3. **"24 h | 7 days"** shows both options, as `<app-segmented-choice>` (Lars, 2026-11-04). Check its fit in the
   list header at phone and desktop width; record the placement in `docs/design-language.md` if it differs.
4. **Dimming** combines the existing read-state mark (the unread dot follows the story) with a new token,
   `--viewed-story-opacity`; the read-state styling alone (a dot) does not read as "dimmed" (decision 7).
   Un-ticking "read" on a lead does not un-dim a story the server already called viewed; acceptable (viewed is
   one-way, #307).
5. **Next/previous.** The app has no next/previous-article control today (grep `nextEntry|previousEntry|neighbou`
   finds only `ReaderRouteState.prefetchNeighbours`). Because the list holds only leads, any such control built
   on `EntriesStore.entries()` walks the leads; nothing Trending-specific is needed. Verify the grep is still empty.
6. **Thresholds in copy.** The empty-state sentences name 3 and 5, duplicating the backend's `TrendingWindow`
   enum. If tuning changes them, change both dictionaries.
7. **Boy-scout candidate, verify live:** pressing Enter on an existing "Also in" chip may bubble to the card's
   `(keydown.enter)` (`OpensEntryDirective`) and open the lead as well as the popover. If so, add the same
   `(keydown.enter)`/`(keydown.space)` `stopPropagation()` the new `+N more` button carries, in its own commit,
   named in the PR.
8. **Fixtures.** Do not paper over a body missing `trending` with a store fallback (Task 11); fix the fixture.
9. **Planner.** No change (Task 14's check). If the live check shows Trending pages visibly taller than others,
   the fix is a footer allowance in `pageHeight` for entries with a non-empty `alsoFootOf(entry).copies` — raise
   it with Lars rather than tuning `PAGE_HEIGHT_CAP`.
